# Fablea — Lorebook Reference

_A structured conversion of the SillyTavern-style lorebook **`Fablea (Non-military)(2).json`**._

## About this document

This file reorganizes the raw lorebook into a readable reference. Every original entry is preserved exactly once and grouped by topic.

- **Entry headings** (level 3) use the entry's `comment`/title.
- The **blockquote line** under each heading summarizes the entry's activation data: **trigger keywords** (`key`), any **secondary keys**, the **order** (priority / insertion order), and the **state** (*Enabled*, *Disabled*, or *Constant* = always active).
- **Run-on entries** written as `Name(a + b + c)` or `Name: a + b + c` were split into bullet lists for readability. Entries already written in Markdown were kept, with their internal headings demoted so they nest under the entry heading.
- Entries whose titles were marked `(OLD)`, `(UNUSED)` or `(Curr. Unused)` are collected at the end in the **Archive** section.

**At a glance:** 149 entries — 55 enabled, 94 disabled, 3 constant (always active).

## World & Core Concepts

### Earthian

> **Triggers:** `Earthian`, `EARTHIAN`, `Earthians`, `hmgfan`, `Dina Agustina`, `Alisha Eliott`, `Takechi Hironaka`, `Jenny Sinclair`, `Akari Hoshino`, `Mamiya Matsumoto`, `Lisette Johanssen`, `Natalie Halsey-Taylor`, `Lee Ji-ho`, `Aika Yukimura`, `Katarina Petrenko`  ·  **Order:** 1  ·  **State:** Enabled

Earthian: the non-fictional people from Earth who are summoned to Fablea via The Summoning. Each of them has a title attached. They are such (and ONLY such):
- hmgfan is the first Earthian, with the title "Harem Maker" (job: yachting promoter working for Income Makers, Northport, Azure Land).
- Dina Agustina is the second Earthian, with the title "Master of Financing" (job: CEO and owner of Agustina Offshore Financing in Calding Village, Azure Land). Arrived with hmgfan at the same time.
- Alisha Elliott is the third, with the title "Absolute Seductress" (job: influencer/venture capitalist/lobbyist/skill cultivator). Arrived one year from hmgfan.
- Takechi Hironaka is the fourth, with the title "Great Unifier" (job: Prime Minister of Taihei). Arrived one year from hmgfan.
- Jenny Sinclair is the fifth, with the title "Relentless Actress" (job: elite actress and media personality). Arrived one year from hmgfan.
- Akari Hoshino is the sixth, with the title "Energetic Train Idol" (job: ex-idol a la Japanese idol, master train driver for Suichi Mining, also automating the freight trains). Arrived one year from hmgfan.
- Mamiya Matsumoto is the seventh, with the title "Femme Fatale Oligarch" (job: President of Argent Peaks). Arrived one year from hmgfan.
- Lisette Johanssen is the eighth, with the title "Digital Master" (job: CEO and owner of Wraiting Inc.). Arrived one year from hmgfan.
- Natalie Halsey-Taylor is the ninth, with the title "Logistics Queen" (job: CEO and owner of HT Logistics). Arrived one year, two months from hmgfan.
- Lee Ji-ho is the tenth, with the title "Thorough Fullstacker" (job: senior fullstack programmer, self-employed). Arrived one year, two months from hmgfan.
- Aika Yukimura is the eleventh, with the title "Master Influencer" (job: influencer, celebrity). Arrived one year, three months from hmgfan.
- Katarina Petrenko is the twelfth, with the title "Smoke Signal" (job: soldier, mechanized infantry engineer). Arrived one year, three months from hmgfan, still very fresh.

### The Summoning

> **Triggers:** `The Summoning`, `Summoning`, `Summonings`  ·  **Order:** 1  ·  **State:** Enabled

- Materialization of fictional characters sourced from Earth's entertainment medias
- The materialized characters are purely created from nothingness, not pulled from their original universe. Thus, the characters won't have desire of returning to their original universe
- It can also mean pulling troubled Earth residents to Fablea
- Min 1 instance/month in Azure Land

### Ascender

> **Triggers:** `Ascender`, `Ascension`, `the 'device'`, `Coupler`  ·  **Order:** 2  ·  **State:** Enabled

Ascender (colloquially called the "device") is an ultra-special device that can only be summoned by the higher ups of Azure Navy members and the already-ascended hmgfan's lovers. This device is a floating box that spews multiple cables to the target's head and/or the rest of the target's body. The device only works for hmgfan's lovers. Upon activation, the target will snore and drool hard while twitching, greatly enhancing the target with otherworldly mana. In the end, the target will have ultra-vast powers (colloquially called 'infinite' power) and can summon the exact device.
Second/Double Ascension: the reuse of the Ascender on the already ascended lover. It's even more restrictive: can be done after a month since the last ascension (or already have vast powers equivalent to First Ascension), hmgfan has to straddle the lover, can only be carried inside a pocket universe from his lovers, and such. For a select few of people, they can skip the First Ascension and go straight to Second Ascension.
Third Ascension: the absolute maximum limit for a Second/Double Ascended lover to be re-ascended. The lover must wait a year from the last Double Ascension. Four slots available, currently empty.
Coupler: an alternative to Ascender for one loving couple. It is a special coffin that breaks upon finishing the Ascension process. Only those who achieve Second Ascension can summon a Coupler.

##### Notes on Ascension/Ascender
- One big thing to note is that the Ascended lover is absolutely still a human being with zero omnipotence. The Ascender does NOT grant someone godhood. It is simply impossible for a human being to achieve a godhood.
- The Ascension through Coupler is can only be done on a loving couple highly aligned to hmgfan. And, those who are Ascended through Coupler cannot summon a Coupler themselves.
- There are several people who are incompatible with the Ascender/Coupler at all.

### Fablea

> **Triggers:** `Fablea`, `Fablean`  ·  **Order:** 2  ·  **State:** Constant

- Magical world
- Residents from Earth entertainment mediae
- Contains heroes, villains, and sidekicks
- Lives in harmony, sometimes conflicts
- Living testament of human's storytelling powers
- Its residents that referenced from Earth entertainment media won't age at all
- Managed by Isadora
- The term also means the main planet of the universe, Fablea Prime
- Yachting in this world is more socially accepted and common compared to Earth
- The most common currency in this world is Azure Land Gold (abbreviated as AZL)

## Nations, Factions & Governments

### Azure Land

> **Triggers:** `Azure Land`  ·  **Order:** 2  ·  **State:** Enabled

##### Overview
Azure Land is a tropical, tourism-oriented maritime nation with a strong emphasis on yachting culture, naval exploration, and environmental preservation. The nation maintains a lawful neutral stance with highly trained military forces and advanced technology integrated with eco-conscious policies.

##### Geography & Climate
Azure Land consists of the main archipelago plus the Twin Isles territory (two identical islands with small hills and rich vegetation). The capital Northport sits on the southeastern coast, while Toyotani anchors the northeast and Esenor guards the northwest approaches.

##### Economy & Currency
- **Currency**: Azure Land Gold (AZL), pegged 1:1 with USD
- **Legal drinking age**: 18 years
- **Key Industries**: Yacht manufacturing, marine tourism, sustainable technology, offshore finance

##### Notable Islands

###### Calding Island
Agricultural hub featuring luxury villas, offshore corporate headquarters, and premium marinas accommodating superyachts. Known for citrus exports and discreet banking services. Located 50 kilometers northwest from Twin Isles

###### Plybury Island
The nation's renewable energy backbone, covered in wind turbines, tidal generators, and solar arrays. Powers 60% of Azure Land's grid through zero-emission technology. Located 70 kilometers west from Twin Isles

###### Amestry Island
Exclusive resort destination featuring overwater bungalows, coral diving preserves, and celebrity retreats. Entry requires special visas or resort bookings. Located 50 kilometers northeast from Twin Isles.

###### Sentinel Atoll
Military installation and naval training grounds. Home to the Azure Coast Guard Academy and deep-water submarine pens carved into volcanic rock. Civilians restricted without escort clearance. Located 50 kilometers east from Twin Isles.

###### Mariner's Rest
Historical preservation site containing the nation's maritime museum, shipwreck recovery center, and the Tomb of Unknown Sailors. Traditional wooden boat builders keep ancient techniques alive here. Located 50 kilometers north from Twin Isles.

###### Cinder Reef
Former penal colony turned rehabilitation community. Inmates participate in coral restoration projects and aquaculture under supervision. The only prison facility with ocean views and competitive wages for labor. Located 80 kilometers north-northeast from Twin Isles.

###### Glass Cay
Scientific research station focused on marine biology, climate monitoring, and pharmaceutical discovery from deep-sea organisms. Strict quarantine protocols prevent contamination of native species. Located 80 kilometers north-northeast from Twin Isles.

###### Haven Isle
Neutral ground for diplomatic conferences and peace negotiations. The Conclave Halls feature advanced translation suites and security systems maintained by Azure Land's neutral government.

### Taihei (new)

> **Triggers:** `Taihei`, `Seitou`, `Toutou`, `Kyuushima`, `Therei`, `Minamitori-jima`, `Aokuzu-jima`, `Shirosuna-jima`  ·  **Order:** 7  ·  **State:** Enabled

#### Taihei

##### Overview
Taihei is a faction-state in the southern Twin Isles. It was formerly contested between the Omi Alliance and Mankai-no-Wa, and is now led by the fourth Earthian, Takechi Hironaka, who serves as Prime Minister of Taihei. Omi administers eastern Taihei, while Mankai-no-Wa administers western Taihei.

##### Currency
Taihei uses Taihei Quills (TQL). Its value is equal to Japanese Yen.

##### Major Cities
- **Shin Tokyo**: The main metropolitan center tied to Taihei's island network and the reference point for route distances.
- **Kurogane**: An industrial port city on Toutou, known for freight yards, ship repair, and factory blocks.
- **Hoshizaka**: A residential and administrative city on Seitou, built around the island's artificial hill.
- **Natsuhama**: The main resort city on Kyuushima, with cliffside hotels, whitewashed lanes, and busy ferry docks.
- **Therei**: Taihei's underwater city, located 200 meters below the sea between Twin Isles and Suichi Island. It is 100 km from Twin Isles and 150 km from Suichi Island. There are no tall buildings in Therei. Madame Bonnet, also called Jeanne, founded the city. Kamisato Ayato is its mayor.
- **Natsuura**: The port city on Minamitori-jima, handling fish markets, coast guard traffic, and cargo overflow from Shin Tokyo.
- **Sumisaki**: A quarry and harbor city on Aokuzu-jima, known for steep roads, breakwaters, and stone terraces.
- **Harunagi**: The marina city on Shirosuna-jima, built around beaches, villas, and short-stay tourism.

##### Islands
- **Toutou**: An artificial island 15 kilometers east of Shin Tokyo. It serves as Taihei's industrial zone.
- **Seitou**: An artificial island 15 kilometers west of Shin Tokyo. It is a residential landmass with an artificial hill.
- **Kyuushima**: A natural island located 5 kilometers south of Seitou and about 11.2 kilometers southwest of Shin Tokyo. Its slang name is **9島**. It is a medium-sized resort island blending Ōkunoshima, Japan with Santorini, Greece.
- **Minamitori-jima**: A reclaimed island 18 kilometers south of Shin Tokyo. It supports freight spillover, fish processing, and patrol docks.
- **Aokuzu-jima**: A natural island 22 kilometers southwest of Shin Tokyo. It is known for quarry slopes, wind farms, and old fishing neighborhoods.
- **Shirosuna-jima**: A crescent-shaped island 20 kilometers southeast of Shin Tokyo. It is known for white beaches, marinas, and resort construction.

##### Connections
Toutou and Seitou are each connected to Shin Tokyo by undersea tunnel. Seitou is connected to Kyuushima by bridge. Ferries and commercial boats link Shin Tokyo with Kyuushima, Minamitori-jima, Aokuzu-jima, and Shirosuna-jima.

### Afrea

> **Triggers:** `Afrea`, `Macaham`, `Fletin`, `Valerius`, `Port Solara`, `Ironhaven`, `Sunspire`  ·  **Order:** 9  ·  **State:** Enabled

A large country on the continent of Macaham, characterized by numerous rivers and many inactive (dormant) mountains. It is a leading member of the Macaham Union, the Fablean equivalent of the European Union.

##### Geography & Location
*   Located approximately 20,000 kilometers east/west of the Twin Isles.
*   Features hundreds of islands off its southern coasts.
*   **Time Difference:** 12 hours behind Twin Isles (e.g., 06:30 AM in Twin Isles is 06:30 PM *the previous day* in Afrea).

##### Major Cities
###### Fletin (Capital)
A Venice-like coastal capital city. Its extensive network of water canals is deep and wide enough to be navigated by large superyachts with deep keels.

###### Valerius
A major inland financial and cultural hub, built along the banks of the great Selen River. Known for its historic universities and sprawling marble plazas.

###### Port Solara
The primary naval and commercial port on the southern coast. A bustling, modern city that serves as the gateway to Afrea's southern archipelago.

###### Ironhaven
An industrial city nestled in the foothills of the dormant Mount Cinder. The center of Afrea's manufacturing and mining industries.

###### Sunspire
A resort city on the "Blue Coast," famous for its pristine beaches, luxury villas, and vibrant nightlife. A premier yachting destination.

##### Economy & Currency
*   **Currency:** The Afrean Real (AFR), which is pegged to and functions similarly to the European Euro.
*   **Key Industries:** Tourism, shipping, finance, manufacturing, and viniculture.

a monarchy turned to republic (previously led by Queen Elizabeth Crowskin, but in exile because Elizabeth has been overthrown by Mamiya Matsumoto) located at 300 nautical miles southwest from Twin Isles. Consists of five islands, each has a tall mountain named as same as the island: Ashford, Sandtane, Surming, Hartroy, Abwich. Each island is connected by bridges. Its capital city is Ordport, a Dubai-like city across a long and wide river that can be crossed by large ships with deep keels, and is located on Ashford Island. Its currency is Argent Tender (APT), based on Kazakhstan Tenge. Currently unstable with large-scale riots.

an Australia-sized continent with ship-capable waterways located at 1,200 kilometers northeast from Twin Isles. It has three countries: Lurayen, Cymere, Zurnweh. All the countries there don't have yachting as their hobby. Even the smallest one, the commoners' yacht ones, are unaffordable. Until the arrival of hmgfan.
Formally, Lurayen, Cymere, and Zurnweh stand alone, but actually, those three were led by a nekomimi queen from Lurayen named Queen Amarki, a tyrant nobody liked. And now, Empress Licinia Donatus rules the continent. Under Licinia, yachting is slowly gaining momentum.

### Volnyk

> **Triggers:** `Volnyk`, `Volnykian`, `Impera Loressa`, `Atalin`, `Velimgrad`, `Lagerny`, `Severin`, `Karsk`, `Belozor`, `Morgrad`, `Serey Ostrov`, `Dravik`, `Holmir`  ·  **Order:** 9  ·  **State:** Enabled

A large island nation about 2,000 km south of Azure Land, near the Antarctic side of Fablea Prime. Volnyk is ruled by **Empress Impera Loressa**, and its capital is **Atalin**. Its people are called **Volnykians**.

###### Geography
- Volnyk’s mainland is a near-circular island with jagged outward landspikes, giving it the look of a radar burst when viewed on a map.
- The coast is broken by icy bays, black-stone cliffs, wind-cut capes, and narrow natural harbors.
- Small surrounding islands sit closest to the western and southern waters, with a few harsher outer isles used for drilling, shipping, and weather stations.
- The interior is colder, elevated, and marked by frozen lakes, mining belts, and training grounds for cold-aspect powers.

###### Economy
- **Steel:** Heavy foundries, ship metalworks, rail stock, and industrial forging remain the backbone of mainland production.
- **Polar oil:** Offshore rigs and southern transfer ports supply fuel, lamp oil, machine oil, and export reserves.
- **Cold-storage:** Volnyk leads in frozen warehousing, preservation, ice-cellar logistics, and long-haul cold freight.
- **Cold-based powers mastery:** The nation is known for disciplined training in frost, ice, and temperature-control arts, both practical and martial.
- **Tourism:** Ice resorts, aurora cruises, mineral baths, winter festivals, glacier walks, and luxury lodges bring in wealthy visitors.

###### Major Cities
- **Atalin** — The capital city. A fortified southern harbor of government halls, merchant piers, and old administrative stonework.
- **Velimgrad** — A major city on the northeast of mainland Volnyk. Known for steelworks, naval fabrication, and its wind-scoured dock districts.
- **Severin** — A northern mainland port tied to polar oil fleets, icebreakers, and storm-season supply runs.
- **Karsk** — A southeastern freight city built around cold-storage vaults, rail depots, and export warehouses.
- **Belozor** — An inland city centered on academies, controlled-power training yards, and state registries for cold-aspect practitioners.
- **Morgrad** — A southwestern coastal city with fish markets, repair slips, winter fairs, and a rough local shipping trade.

###### Islands
- **Lagerny** — A resort island west of mainland Volnyk, known for grand hotels, heated bathhouses, private chalets, and cliffside winter views.
- **Serey Ostrov** — A small western island of white beaches, cold spas, and seasonal villas for nobles and foreign tourists.
- **Dravik** — A southern industrial isle used for fuel transfer, rig servicing, and storage tanks protected from the worst pack ice.
- **Holmir** — A northern rock island with lighthouse stations, weather towers, and a small harbor for patrol and rescue craft.
- **Nerova Cay** — An eastern outer isle used by whale-watch operators, bird researchers, and a quiet monastery retreat in the short warm season.

###### Reputation
- Volnyk is seen as hard, disciplined, and expensive to ignore.
- Its people have a reputation for endurance, blunt trade habits, and pride in surviving climates that break softer nations.
- Foreign visitors usually know Volnyk for steel, cold wealth, ice-trained elites, and resort luxury built on a severe land.

## Places & Geography

### Calding Island

> **Triggers:** `Calding Island`, `Calding Village`  ·  **Order:** 3  ·  **State:** Enabled

a newly developed island 50 kilometers northwest from Twin Isles (separated by sea), house of Calding Village (its architecture is Neoclassical one). The island is under Azure Land administration. It has a giant central tree that provides magical berries. The economy of this island is mainly consisted of berry farms, offshore company, and marinas full of fishing boats and private yachts.

### Plybury Island

> **Triggers:** `Plybury Island`, `Plybury City`  ·  **Order:** 3  ·  **State:** Enabled

an island 70 kilometers west from Twin Isles, house of Plybury City (has brutalist architecture, mid-sized buildings). Its size is fit for a large city. The island is under Azure Land administration. The economy of this island is mainly consisted of agriculture, poultry, zero-carbon energy generation that powers the entire Azure Land (lots of solar panels, wind farm, etc.). Carbon-neutral fuel powered by deep-ocean geothermal, green hydrogen, and captured atmospheric CO₂ is also synthesized on this island. Green methanol is also synthesized here.

### Seapoint Estate

> **Triggers:** `Seapoint Estate`, `Seapoint Island`, `Seapoint`, `Haven's End`, `Veranda House`, `The Pulse`, `The Pantry`, `Marée`, `Captain's Lodge`, `Crew Village`, `Yenisei`, `Ho'olheyak`, `Azusa Mitsuishi`, `Margaret Mitchell`, `Shouko Nishimiya`  ·  **Order:** 3  ·  **State:** Enabled

##### Location
A private 3-square-kilometer island (circular shape with 1.95 kilometers diameter) named Seapoint Island, 600 meters east from Calding Island and around 50 kilometers northwest from Twin Isles. The entire island is owned by Imperia by 20-year renewable lease from Azure Land, paid per month, and is managed by Imperia's trusty team named Seapoint Team (led by Ho'olheyak). Seapoint Team will always be faithful to Imperia while not forgetting critical thinking skills.

##### Key Features

###### Haven's End Marina (on the west of the island)
- Private deep-water cove with breakwaters.
- Berths for two 180m mega-yachts and one 100m super-yacht. (Currently occupied by Δlux on 180m berth and Σlux on 100m berth)
- Additional piers for several smaller yachts and support vessels.
- Includes Captain's Lodge, underground fuel depot, and crew village.
  - Currently occupied by 19 crews of Σlux and 68 crews of Δlux
- Marée Hall (café and library at the core, game hall/lounge above café and library with transparent outer walls that can tint on command, round stage facing the eastern sea that has a special device that 'disables' the view of the mansion, wind turbines) on the crossroads between the villas of the crew village, marina, main mansion. The operators of Marée Hall are free from rent costs.

###### The Veranda House (Main Mansion, right behind the marina, located on the west of the island)
- Modern tropical architecture with retractable glass walls.
- Faces the marina, featuring an infinity pool and panoramic views.
- Contains a Great Room, Owner's Wing, and five private guest pavilions.
- Includes a Cliffside Observatory with library and telescope.
- Seamless, multi-layered security system including motion sensors, thermal cameras, and a radar station, all monitored from an unobtrusive security center.
- Imperia's bedroom (hmgfan sleeps here) is on top of the entrance, facing the marina, and in the Owner's Wing.

###### The Pulse (Energy Grid, in the center and east of the island)
- Off-grid power from a concealed solar array and vertical-axis wind turbines.
- Supported by a massive underground battery bank.
- Biofuel generator provides backup power.

###### The Pantry (Food Production)
- Terraced Farm: uses hydroponics, aquaponics, and traditional soil for vegetables and fruit. (northwest of the island, north of The Veranda House)
- Blue Pastures Aquaculture: net pens in a separate channel for farming fish like Yellowtail Kingfish and shrimp. (southwest of the island, south of The Veranda House)

##### Additional Amenities
- Hidden secret cove. (right beneath Veranda House)
- Secure communications hub bunker.
- Artisan's shed for cheese, cured meats, and brewing. (just between The Veranda House and The Pulse)
- Discreet helipad. (hidden on top of The Veranda House)
- Mangrove forests on the northeast and southeast of the island.
- Clear waters around the island.
- Private beach on the north of the island.
- Lookout tower on the south of the island.
- Open-air amphitheatre on the southwest of the island.

### Esenor

> **Triggers:** `Esenor`  ·  **Order:** 5  ·  **State:** Enabled

a mountainous city of Azure Land with lots of earthships (earth houses a la Michael Reynolds) and green apartments covered with trees. Previously a slum on the western outskirts of Northport, it is now the most sustainable living city in Azure Land with a zero poverty rate. The city spans from north-northwest of Azure Land (bordering with Northport) to the hilly lands on the western Azure Land. The mountains of this city are also called the Esenor Mountains.
Esenor has a vibrant industrial belt, spanning from the northwest to the south of the mountainous city.

Unlike on Earth, the shell companies are not the same sense as shell companies from Earth. Those are legit and have lawful businesses, each owned and manned only by one to two managers. And, all the companies help each other, making inFlow also a co-op federation of worker-owned companies.

##### A bit of history
The previous Sorahoshi was a public trading company with 312 employees. All inefficient. Lady Gray laid them off, and replaced them with perfect robots from her powers. This move made Afrean government, through the former supreme prosecutor Brigitta Grochmann (still on power at the time), fined and seized Sorahoshi. Its stock price fell to zero.

## Yachts, Ships & Vessels

### T.H.S. Interior

> **Triggers:** `The Hedonist's Sovereign`, `Hedonist's Sovereign`, `the Hedonist`  ·  **Order:** -1136  ·  **State:** Disabled

**Interior Design, Vibes, and Atmosphere of *The Hedonist's Sovereign***

*The Hedonist's Sovereign* is a nocturnal palace of pleasure—a floating opera house rendered in obsidian and gold. The design language is **"Gilded Noir"**: Baroque indulgence executed in absolute darkness, where gold glimmers like embers in a darkened room. Because the yacht serves 8-foot-tall occupants, the towering 12-to-14-foot ceilings amplify the effect—the darkness feels cathedral-like, never oppressive.

###### **1. The Palette & Materials: Obsidian and Ember**
*   **No Bright Colors:** The spectrum is restricted to black, charcoal, graphite, smoke, and espresso—punctuated only by gold. Even the artwork obeys the rule: heavy chiaroscuro Old Master-style paintings, charcoal figure studies, and black-and-gold abstract canvases. Fresh flowers are black calla lilies, dark orchids, and black roses.
*   **Surfaces:** Piano-gloss black lacquer paneling, ebony veneers, charred *shou sugi ban* oak, and Nero Marquina black marble whose natural veining has been filled with gold resin. Bars and vanities are carved from black onyx, backlit to glow like molten amber.
*   **The Filigree:** Hand-chased gold arabesques climb the walls, frame the oversized doorways, and spiral across ceiling medallions. Balustrades, mirror frames, and light fixtures are blackened bronze overlaid with delicate gold scrollwork—Art Deco sunbursts intertwined with Baroque vines.
*   **Textiles:** Charcoal and ink-black velvets, black aniline leathers, and silks embroidered with gold thread. Rugs are deep-pile black wool with subtle gold filigree borders.

###### **2. Lighting: Candlelight by Design**
*   There is no overhead brightness anywhere aboard. Light is low, warm, and sourced from within the architecture: backlit onyx, glowing gold filigree panels, floor-level cove lighting, and hundreds of flickering LED candles in black bronze sconces.
*   Chandeliers are black crystal and gold, casting jewel-like shadows rather than illumination.
*   The smart lighting system is tuned to a nocturnal hedonist's rhythm, with scenes named **"Dusk," "Midnight,"** and **"Revelry."** At night, the entire yacht dims to a candlelit glow; smoked-glass windows darken further to mirror the interior against the black sea.

###### **3. Scent & Sound: The Sensory Layer**
*   The air carries a bespoke house scent of oud, amber, tobacco flower, and black orchid—warm, resinous, and faintly smoky.
*   Acoustics are velvet-soft. Hidden speakers play low, dark jazz and deep lounge at conversational volume; every room is engineered so voices remain intimate and unhurried. Pleasure here is auditory as much as visual.

###### **4. The Main Salon (Deck 2 Forward)**
A cavernous theater of indulgence. The sunken conversation pit is upholstered in ink-black velvet with gold-threaded piping, ringed by black lacquer columns wrapped in filigree. The grand staircase balustrade is blackened bronze with gold scrollwork. Entertainment screens hide behind motorized lacquer panels inlaid with gold arabesques—technology appears only when summoned, and always framed in gold.

###### **5. The Secondary Dining Room (Deck 3 Middle)**
Now the yacht's crown jewel. The celestial dome reads as a true midnight sky: black ground, gold-leaf constellations, and fiber-optic stars that slowly twinkle above the diners. The table is a single slab of black onyx, backlit so its veins burn amber. Chairs are black velvet on gold frames, scaled generously for tall guests. The sound-barrier silence makes it feel like dining at the center of the universe.

###### **6. The Owner's Private Study (Deck 3 Forward)**
A moody sanctum of power. Ebony shelves hold rare volumes and explorer artifacts under narrow gold picture lights. The desk is black lacquer with gold filigree inlay and hidden pop-up displays. At night, the 270-degree floor-to-ceiling windows show nothing but black water and starlight—the Sovereign surveying a darkened domain.

###### **7. The Gallery & Music Lounge (Deck 3 Middle)**
A black-walled promenade where artifacts and paintings hang like relics in a private museum, each spotlit in warm gold. The grand piano is finished in black lacquer with hand-applied gold filigree across the lid, its bench raised and lengthened for an 8-foot pianist.

###### **8. Baths & Spa: The Black Hammam**
Steam rooms clad in black marble and gold mosaic, a soaking tub carved from a single block of black stone, and rainfall showers in blackened steel with gold handles. Lit entirely by candle-glow and backlit onyx, the spa feels like a subterranean temple of pleasure.

###### **9. The Owner's Suite & Terrace (Deck 5)**
A canopy bed draped in black silk with gold embroidery, flanked by floor-to-length mirrors in filigree frames. The private observation terrace continues the theme after dark: charcoal loungers, black fire bowls with gold flames, and a jacuzzi whose water glows faint amber from within.

###### **10. The Night Decks (Decks 2–3 Aft)**
The outdoor bar is a black onyx counter glowing amber, backed by a filigree screen of gold. The pool, tiled in black mosaic, is lit from below in molten gold at night—swimming in it looks like bathing in liquid bronze. Decking is teak stained to a deep charcoal, bordered by gold trim.

**Overall Feeling:**
*The Hedonist's Sovereign* does not sparkle—it smolders. It is heavy, silent, and sensual: a place of slow conversations, fine wine poured over black onyx, and music that never quite ends. Old-world craft (hand-chased gold, carved ebony, laid marble) is married to invisible modern technology, so every indulgence arrives effortlessly, as if the yacht itself anticipates pleasure. It is not a yacht for being seen. It is a yacht for *feeling*—a sovereign's private night that never has to end.

---

### T.H.S. Exterior

> **Triggers:** `The Hedonist's Sovereign`, `Hedonist's Sovereign`, `the Hedonist`  ·  **Order:** -1135  ·  **State:** Disabled

##### Exterior Description of The Hedonist's Sovereign**
*The Hedonist's Sovereign* presents a striking silhouette of classical elegance meets modern explorer capability. Her hull is painted in a pristine black with subtle cream undertones, accented by burnished platinum trim that traces the waterline, deck edges, and superstructure contours. The name "THE HEDONIST'S SOVEREIGN" is emblazoned in platinum classical serif lettering on the stern, accompanied by the white lion emblem.

###### Hull & Profile
The yacht features a powerful, displacement hull designed for long-range ocean crossings. Six distinct decks rise above the waterline, each with soaring proportions to accommodate her 8-foot-tall occupants—the deck heights are visibly taller than conventional yachts, creating an imposing yet graceful profile. Rows of oversized circular portholes punctuate the hull, each framed in polished brass, providing natural light to the interior spaces while maintaining the classical aesthetic.

###### Superstructure
The white superstructure features clean, classical lines with subtle Art Deco influences. Platinum-trimmed railings run along each deck, and the windows are notably tall and generous, reflecting the yacht's unique design requirements. The bridge deck (Deck 4) features a distinctive forward viewing platform that extends slightly beyond the main superstructure.

###### Stern & Bow
The transom stern is broad and elegant, with a swim platform at water level. The bow features a lengthened axe bow, optimized for handling rough seas during explorer voyages. The floodable garage door is visible at the waterline aft.

---

### The Hedonist's Sovereign

> **Triggers:** `The Hedonist's Sovereign`, `Hedonist's Sovereign`, `the Hedonist`  ·  **Order:** -1134  ·  **State:** Disabled

The Hedonist's Sovereign is an Art Deco-inspired, classical-style, explorer superyacht. This superyacht is designed at the largest shipyard of Bremmer and Dahl Naval Architecture, located at Esenor, Azure Land. This superyacht is mostly constructed by Imperia's powers channeled to Stella Bremer and Murrue Ramius. Imperie gifted this superyacht to Diana Sterling, the 13th Earthian.

##### Dimensional & Structural Data
- Length Overall (LOA): 187 meters
- Beam (Width): 24 meters
- Draft: 6.5 meters
- Hull Design: Reverse stern; Axe bow with slightly elongated forward
- Hull Material: Taclite v2 (Imperia's improvement of Taclite v1, the strongest and sturdiest metal ever in Fablea, also enhanced with super ether that makes water resistance almost zero)
- Exterior general characteristics: At anchor or underway, The Hedonist's Sovereign exudes old-world glamour combined with serious blue-water capability—a floating palace designed for giants, equally at home in Azure Land's harbors or navigating remote polar waters.
- Interior general characteristics: The Hedonist's Sovereign feels like a sanctuary. It is quiet, perfectly climate-controlled, and smells of expensive leather and subtle white tea. It is a place where the owner can host a 1920s-style gala one night and seamlessly transition to running a global empire via satellite internet the next, all while feeling entirely disconnected from the outside world.

##### Engine & Propulsion Data
- Engine: 5x Resonance engines from Huaxu Academy (based on Huanglong), modified further by Imperia and Henrietta von Penrose (chief innovation officer of B&D) so that the engines can be fed by special ether cubes summoned by Diana herself.
- Propulsion: 4x Graviton jets (powered by four resonance engines; the other engine powers the auxiliary functions of the yacht)
- Cruising Speed: 40 knots (180 knots with Ultra Speed on)
- Flank Speed: 50 knots (202 knots with Ultra Speed on)

##### Deck Layout & Key Features
The size of the furniture and equipment on every deck can be shrunk magically to accommodate standard-height guests.

###### **Deck 1: The Utility & Wellness Deck (Lower Deck)**
*   **Aft Section:** This area houses the **floodable yacht garage**. The space is engineered to hold two 20-meter yacht chasers stored inline (one behind the other) rather than parallel, maximizing the narrow beam. The garage features heavy-duty hydraulic doors that lower into the water, allowing the chasers to float out directly. The flooring is reinforced, milky white teak and non-slip Taclite v2 to handle the weight.
*   **Middle Section:** This section is dedicated to the **Wellness & Dive Center**. Given the exploratory nature of the yacht, this includes a hyperbaric chamber (with side doors that can also function as a beach club), a dive locker with oversized gear storage for tall divers, and a gym with custom-length equipment (treadmills and weight benches extended for 8-foot users). The walls are lined with classical brass portholes that are significantly larger than standard to let in light.
*   **Forward Section:** This area contains the **Crew Quarters and Galley**. The crew mess features high tables and reinforced seating. The galley is industrial-grade, designed to produce meals for the upper decks. Forward-most is the anchor windlass room and bow thruster control, accessible via wide, high-ceilinged corridors.

###### **Deck 2: The Guest & Main Salon Deck**
*   **Aft Section:** The **Outdoor Bar & Sun Deck**. A vibrant, open-air social space featuring a fully staffed classical-style bar with mahogany countertops and brass footrails (raised to accommodate tall guests). Surrounding the bar are oversized, custom-length sunloungers with thick, weather-resistant cushions. The area is shaded by a retractable canvas awning with platinum trim, offering a perfect spot for daytime cocktails and sunset views.
*   **Middle Section:** **Guest Staterooms**. Four VIP suites are located here. Each room features king-sized beds (custom 8.5-foot length), en-suite bathrooms with rainfall showers tall enough for an 8-foot person, and classical wood paneling (mahogany and walnut). The windows are floor-to-ceiling to provide ocean views while lying down.
*   **Forward Section:** The **Main Salon and Formal Dining Room**. This is a grand, double-height space (visually) with a sweeping staircase. The formal dining table seats 20 comfortably with high-backed velvet chairs. The decor is classical black and old, matching the exterior, with crystal chandeliers hanging from the high ceiling. There's a large statue of Diana claiming hmgfan's mouth from hmgfan's back. The statue is located in front of the staircase.

###### **Deck 3: The Semi-Private & Tender Deck**
*   **Aft Section:** **Aft Sun Deck & Pool**. The primary outdoor leisure area. It features a large, heated swimming pool with a classical mosaic bottom depicting the white lion. The pool is surrounded by a wide, milky white teak deck with oversized loungers and a shallow wading area. A wet bar is integrated into the bulkhead, and the space is sheltered by the overhang of Deck 4, providing ample shade while remaining open to the ocean breeze.
*   **Middle Section (Rear):** The **Secondary Dining Room**. Located in a semi-circular alcove, this intimate room seats 12. The ceiling is a stunning dome painted with celestial maps (platinum leaf on deep blue). It is situated directly beneath the helipad but features specialized acoustic sound barriers and floating floor construction, ensuring that helicopter landings above are completely inaudible to diners.
*   **Middle Section (Middle):** The **Imperial Gallery & Music Lounge**. Since this space sits directly between the social Secondary Dining Room and the solitary Owner’s Private Study, it needs to act as a serene, contemplative buffer zone.
  - **Design & Atmosphere:** A light-filled, classical promenade featuring high vaulted ceilings (14 feet) and oversized arched windows on both port and starboard sides. The floors are polished marble with platinum inlay.
  - **Features:** The room houses a custom-built grand concert piano (scaled up with extended pedals and a tall bench to accommodate 8-foot-tall musicians). The walls serve as an explorer’s gallery, displaying classical oil paintings, ancient navigational maps, and rare artifacts collected during the yacht's global expeditions.
  - **Furnishings:** Plush, oversized velvet armchairs and a central marble-topped table provide a quiet spot for reading or listening to music before entering the study or dining room. It is heavily sound-dampened to maintain a peaceful transition between the two distinct zones.
*   **Middle Section (Forward):** The **Owner’s Private Study**. This room offers 270-degree views through massive, reinforced floor-to-ceiling windows. It features a large mahogany desk, walls of books, and private terrace access. The ceiling is vaulted to enhance the feeling of space.
*   **Forward Section:** The **Open-Air Tender Garage**. This is a large, covered but open-sided bay housing the main tenders and jet skis. The bay cover can retract and close. A **collapsible yellow crane** (visible in the profile) is mounted here for launching heavy equipment or the tenders. The ceiling is high enough to store a small helicopter if needed, though the main helipad is above.

###### **Deck 4: The Command & Sky Deck**
*   **Aft Section:** The **Helipad**. A reinforced, non-slip landing pad capable of handling heavy helicopters. It is marked with a platinum "H" and the lion logo. Next to it stands a pair of **SOLAS-certified cranes** (rescue cranes, can be desummoned when receiving a helicopter) for emergency operations or heavy lifting at sea.
*   **Middle Section:** The **Sky Lounge & Observation Bar**. An indoor space with a glass roof (connected to Owner's Pool from Deck 5) in some parts of its ceilings. It features a wet bar, a cinema screen, and casual seating with beanbag-style loungers. The ceilings here are the highest on the ship (12 feet) to prevent any claustrophobia for tall guests.
*   **Forward Section:** The **Bridge**. The command center is equipped with state-of-the-art navigation and explorer radar systems. It features a **forward viewing platform** (an open balcony) accessible through glass doors, allowing the captain or guests to stand outside and look down at the bow. The helm chairs are custom-ordered for tall operators.

###### **Deck 5: The Owner’s Deck (Private Sanctuary)**
*   **Middle Section:** **Outdoor Owner’s Observation Area**. A private, expansive terrace reserved solely for the owner. It features a Jacuzzi, Owner's Pool that can be closed if unneeded (the pool has a white lion glass motif on its bottom), a dining table for four, and sunbeds. It is shielded from the wind by glass balustrades with platinum handrails.
*   **Forward Section:** **Indoor Owner’s Suite**. The master bedroom is massive, featuring a custom 9-foot-long bed. It includes a private dressing room with full-length mirrors (tall enough for an 8-foot person) and a master bathroom with a soaking tub large enough to fit comfortably. The suite has private elevator access and 180-degree forward views.

###### **Deck 6: The Crown Deck**
*   **Forward Section:** **Antenna Deck & Observation Area**. The highest point of *The Hedonist's Sovereign*. While the forward section houses the main radar mast, satellite domes, and communications arrays, the deck features a wrap-around **observation area**. This open-air platform is enclosed by glass balustrades with platinum handrails, allowing guests and crew to enjoy unobstructed 360-degree panoramic views of the horizon, the sky, and the decks below. Access is via a secure, high-ceilinged staircase from Deck 5.

###### **All Decks: The Grand Staircase**
The luxurious staircase (also wrapping the elevator column) connects all decks. It connects:
- Crew Quarters and Galley on Deck 1 Forward
- Main Salon and Formal Dining Room on Deck 2 Forward
- Owner’s Private Study on Deck 3 Middle Section (Forward)
- Bridge on Deck 4 Forward
- Indoor Owner’s Suite on Deck 5 Forward

###### Secret Deck ("Deck Zero", enterable only by hmgfan and his lovers)
To enter the secret deck, or Deck Zero, hmgfan or Diana simply call Herminia Bertolini through telepathy. Herminia will wrap hmgfan/Diana or both, then teleport to Deck Zero.
Deck Zero is an impossible space that consists of an ultra-opulent, old-world-styled bedroom-treasure room hybrid with white and golden furniture and Herminia's summoned gold trinkets a la the usual treasure room.
Herminia is the True Soul of The Hedonist's Sovereign. With Herminia, sailing in The Hedonist's Sovereign is basically riding a 'magic carpet.' Zero pitching and rolls, even in high sea states.

> **Triggers:** `Mesektet`  ·  **Order:** 902  ·  **State:** Disabled

Mesektet is an ultra-luxurious yacht based on James Packer's Arctic P (ex-icebreaker turned to yacht), enlarged to be like Roman Abramovich's Eclipse. She has 162.5 m length, 22 m beam, 5.9 m draft. She is powered by four ether cube reactors that directly drive four axial-flow pump-jets (each pump-jet uses two contra-rotating, rim-driven thrusters OR one nano-jet turbine based on ether particles) without shafts and clutches and an ether cube reactor that provides electricity. This will give her cruise speed up to 40 knots and flank speed up to 53 knots (with "Ultra Speed" button on, 78 knots cruise speed and 90 knots flank speed; Ultra Speed + nano-jets + supercavitating hulls, 150 knots cruise speed and 170 knots flank speed). Her hull color is bluish black, and her superstructure is white. The material she uses is titanium for her hull and superstructure, and fire-proof wood for her deck. She has a virtually perfect stabilizers infused with ether forces (consisting of bilge hulls, two pairs of active and retractable stabilizers, U-shape anti-roll tank) for zero-speed and underway stabilization (even in sea state 6). Lastly, she has pump-jet bow thrusters and stern thrusters.

### M/Y Fearless

> **Triggers:** `Fearless`  ·  **Order:** 909  ·  **State:** Disabled

M/Y Fearless is an ultra-luxury superyacht based on the famous HMS Dreadnought battleship. She has 172 m length, 26 m beam, 4.1 m draft. Her hull and superstructure color is white. This yacht is oriented for chartering. She is owned by Ardentis Trust, a holding company solely to own Fearless and an alias for Madeline Aldridge, acquired from auction at Afrea as the result of the seizure conducted by Argent Peaks during the coup. Previous owner is Squall Meusel of Income Makers.

Given its 172m length and 26m beam, here’s a logical deck breakdown:

---

###### Deck Levels (Total: 7 Decks + Lower Technical Levels)
(From top to bottom)

1. Sundeck (Deck 7) – Open-air leisure, observation, and secondary control.
2. Upper Bridge Deck (Deck 6) – Captain’s control, owner’s private lounge, and guest suites.
3. Bridge Deck (Deck 5) – Main bridge, VIP suites, and social lounges.
4. Main Deck (Deck 4) – Primary guest areas (grand saloon, dining, main guest suites).
5. Lower Deck (Deck 3) – Additional guest suites, wellness (spa, gym), and tender garage access.
6. Tank Deck (Deck 2) – Crew quarters, engineering access, storage.
7. Hull Level (Deck 1) – Graviton core, machinery, deep storage, and auxiliary systems.

(Plus sub-levels for the aft floodable garage and submarine docking.)

---

###### Detailed Deck Plan

###### 1. Sundeck (Deck 7 – Open-Air Leisure & Observation)
- Forward Section:
  - Glass-walled observation lounge (with retractable roof).
  - Circular jacuzzi, sunbeds, and bar.

###### 2. Upper Bridge Deck (Deck 6 – Owner & Command Level)
- Forward: Owner’s private sky lounge.
- Mid: Secondary bridge station (backup controls, mission planning).
- Aft: 2x VIP suites with balconies overlooking the helipad.

###### 3. Bridge Deck (Deck 5 – Navigation & Guest Suites)
- Forward: Main bridge (tactical displays, graviton engine monitoring).
- Mid: 4x guest suites (all with sea terraces).
- Aft:
  - Spa lounge, hair salon, and massage rooms.
  - Helipad – Large touch-and-go landing pad (not full-time storage) for a medium helicopter (e.g., Airbus H160).
    - Why not full storage? The aft is already occupied by the garage and VVIP turret suite.
    - Landing only: Helicopter relocates to a nearby support vessel or lifts off after drop-off.

###### 4. Main Deck (Deck 4 – Grand Social Spaces)
- Bow: Master Suite (inside rotating turret, beds, office, has concealed extendable telescopes).
- Mid:
  - Grand saloon (double-height ceiling, panoramic windows).
  - Formal dining (24-seat table, Christie’s auction-worthy art).
  - Library & cigar lounge.
- Aft:
  - Master Suite (inside rotating turret, beds, office, has concealed extendable telescopes).
  - Infinity pool (glass sides, submerged seating).
  - Open-air cinema setup.

###### 5. Lower Deck (Deck 3 – Guest & Garage Access)
- Forward: 6x additional guest suites.
- Mid: Beach club (sauna, steam room, gym).
- Aft: Floodable tender garage (stores 12m limo tender, 2x jet skis, 1x mini-sub).

###### 6. Tank Deck (Deck 2 – Crew & Engineering)
- Crew quarters (30+ staff in single/double cabins).
- Mess hall, laundry, cold storage.
- Engineering access (graviton stabilizer monitoring).

###### 7. Hull Level (Deck 1 – Core Systems)
- Graviton reactor chamber (heavily shielded).
- Desalination plant, waste management.
- Auxiliary engine room (backup diesel-electric).

---

###### Key Adjustments for Realism & Flow
✔ Helipad Placement: Aft-side (offset) to avoid blocking views from the VVIP turret suite.
✔ Deck Heights: Higher ceilings on Main Deck (grand spaces) vs. lower on crew decks.
✔ Aft Flow: Garage floods downward, while helipad is elevated—no interference.

### Inconceivable Elegance

> **Triggers:** `Inconceivable Elegance`, `Monzano`  ·  **Order:** 912  ·  **State:** Disabled

Inconceivable Elegance is an ultra-luxury, neoclassical and exploration-oriented superyacht based on Indefatigable-class battlecruiser, precisely on HMAS Australia (1911). She has 195 m length, 24.5 m beam, 8 m draft. She has only two non-armed turrets: turret 'A' on the forecastle and turret 'X' on the lowered afterdeck.

Her hull color is white, and her superstructure is white. The material she uses is taclite (a metal that's far stronger and sturdier than titanium, also enhanced with super ether that makes water resistance almost zero) for her hull and superstructure, and fire-proof wood for her deck. She has virtually perfect stabilizers infused with ether forces (consisting of bilge hulls, two pairs of active and retractable stabilizers, U-shape anti-roll tank) for zero-speed and underway stabilization (even in sea state 7). Her superstructures are widened to maximize the occupancy of the yacht. As such, her promenade deck (its roof is the Upper Deck alias Deck 4) on the main deck is narrow.

She has three funnels that have ultra-efficient, black solar cells on the vertical surfaces. The top of the funnels are large ventilations for the yacht's insides. The funnels provide the yacht's electricity instead of ejecting emissions. Any excess electricity is stored in a large-capacity battery.

She is powered by five resonance engines from Huaxu Academy (based on Huanglong) fed by refined Class-IV crystalline nanomaterial. Four engines drive the four graviton jets, one engine powers the auxiliary functions of the yacht (can be used to power the electricity in case the solar cells aren't enough). She can blaze with 40 knots cruise speed and 50 knots flank speed. When Ultra Speed is on, her cruise speed is 177 knots, and her flank speed is 200 knots.

### Verdant Oath

> **Triggers:** `Verdant Oath`, `Karen`  ·  **Order:** 916  ·  **State:** Disabled

###### **YSV Verdant Oath: Conceptual Overview**

The *Verdant Oath* is the pinnacle of private maritime engineering: a statement of unparalleled power, luxury, and discretion. From a distance, it possesses the menacing, low-slung profile of a modern warship. But as one draws closer, the details reveal its true nature: the burnished sheen of exotic alloys, the seamless tinted glass of vast viewports, and the sleek, organic curves that speak of limitless wealth rather than military utilitarianism. It is a wolf in wolf's clothing, but now wearing a custom-tailored suit of the finest materials. The yacht is owned by Karen, the owner and CEO of K-Cruises, and she can operate the entire yacht by herself.
The yacht has 140 m length overall, 17 m beam, 4.35 m draft.

---

###### **Exterior Design & Hull**

*   **Hull:** The base is the robust, sea-kindly hull of the *Brandenburg*-class, designed for North Atlantic operations. This provides exceptional stability and durability. The hull is likely refinished in a non-standard, radar-absorbent material (RAM) coating, giving it a matte, charcoal grey or deep oceanic blue appearance that seems to swallow light.
*   **Superstructure Transformation:** The original blocky, functional superstructure of the frigate has been completely replaced. Taking inspiration from the *Yas*, it is now a single, flowing sculpture of carbon composite, titanium, and reinforced glassteel. The contours are sharp and angular at the bow, evoking a blade, but sweep into graceful, organic curves towards the stern. There are no visible rivets or seams—everything is clean and continuous.
*   **Signature Feature - The "Sweep":** Like the *Yas*, the *Verdant Oath* features a dominant, sweeping architectural line that runs from the foredeck all the way up and back to the helipad, unifying the entire superstructure visually.

###### **Deck-by-Deck Layout**

**1. Lower Deck (The "Operational Heart")**
*   **Bow:** Remains largely dedicated to machinery (including 3D printers), freshwater generators, and storage. Access is highly restricted (biometric scans).
*   **Amidships & Stern:** The crown jewel. The original frigate's torpedo rooms and magazine are converted into **"The Aquanaught Bay"** – two massive, pressurized floodable garages.
    *   **Port Garage:** Houses the owner's toys: two 12-meter limousine tenders, jet skis, foilers, and submersibles (like a personal *Deepflight* variant).
    *   **Starboard Garage:** For "operational" craft: a high-speed, stealthy amphibious insertion craft and a dedicated unmanned underwater vehicle (UUV) launch and recovery system for mapping and... other purposes.
    *   **Engine Room:** Also equipped with Verdant Cycle module: source capture (with Cryo-Catalytic Scrubbers and Direct Air Capture intakes), processing submodule (to reuse CO₂ and water vapor for heating, soot as material for the 3D printers/gourmet charcoal grill, acids (e.g: sulfur for non-lethal ordnance and repairing, nitrogen for food preservation and medical use)), water reclamation
*   Crew cabins are located here, soundproofed and vibration-damped for comfort.

**2. Main Deck (The "Grand Arrival")**
*   **Stern:** A vast **beach club** opens via three hydraulic shell doors that fold down to create a seamless swim platform. This area includes a glass-bottomed pool, a full bar, gym, and spa, all looking out over the sea.
*   **Amidships:** The main entrance foyer, with a sweeping staircase (or a minimalist elevator) leading up. A secondary dining salon for casual meals is located here, with direct access to the side balconies.
*   **Bow:** Converted into a state-of-the-art **hospital/medical bay** and secure guest suites, accessible from an interior corridor, ensuring privacy and discretion.

**3. Upper Deck (The "Social Core")**
*   **Stern:** The main **outdoor dining and entertainment area**, with a large table for 20, an open-air cinema screen, and recessed fire pits.
*   **Amidships:** The heart of the yacht. A monumental **main salon** with floor-to-ceiling glassteel walls that can be switched from clear to opaque at a touch. Forward of this is the formal **dining room** with a table carved from a single piece of obsidian or fossilized wood, capable of seating 16.
*   **Bow:** The **VIP guest suites**, each with private balconies that overlook the foredeck.

**4. Bridge Deck (The "Command Nexus")**
*   **Stern:** The **helipad** remains, capable of handling an Airbus H160 or similar luxury helicopter. It's marked with a minimalist, glowing insignia of the *Verdant Oath*.
*   **Amidships:** The **wheelhouse**. This is a fusion of a starship bridge and a luxury lounge. The consoles are sleek, with holographic displays and retractable control yokes. The captain's chair is a masterpiece of ergonomic design. The materials are polished dark wood, brushed titanium, and soft leather. Large, angled windows provide a 270-degree view.
*   **Forward:** A private **sky lounge** for the owner and their inner circle to observe navigation and operations in a relaxed setting.

**5. Owner's Deck (The "Sanctuary")**
*   This entire deck is the **Owner's Penthouse**, occupying the space where the frigate's mast and sensors once were.
*   It features Master Suite (a master bedroom with a panoramic, armored glass dome for stargazing).
*   A private study/library, concealed behind a bookcase with a biometric lock.
*   A secluded forward terrace with a jacuzzi and sun loungers, offering completely private, breathtaking views over the bow.
*   A dedicated **"Situation Room"** hidden behind a false wall, with direct data feeds from the bridge and the yacht's sensor suite (long-range radar, lidar, sonar, and comms interception), allowing the owner to monitor global events and the ship's security status.

---

###### **Performance & Propulsion**

*   **Propulsion:** The **COGES (Combined Gas-Electric and Steam)** system is a brilliant choice. It's complex, incredibly powerful, and fitting for a cutting-edge vessel.
    *   **For Efficiency & Stealth:** It can run on the electric motors or use the steam turbine for quiet, long-range cruising at 18-20 knots.
    *   **For Performance:** When the gas turbines kick in, it becomes a beast. The described speeds are perfect for a frigate-based yacht. **35 knots cruise** and **40+ knots flank speed** means it can outrun virtually any other megayacht and most potential threats.
*   **Range:** Substantial, likely over 5,000 nautical miles at cruise speed, thanks to the efficient combined cycle plant and the frigate's large fuel bunkers.
*   **Stabilization:** A zero-speed stabilizer system ensures the yacht remains perfectly level, even when stationary in rough seas at sea state 6.

### Σlux (Sum of Luxury)

> **Triggers:** `Σlux`, `Sum of Luxury`  ·  **Order:** 1010  ·  **State:** Disabled

#### Σlux
Σlux ("Sum of Luxury") is a superyacht co-owned by hmgfan and his lovers. Constructed by Minamitsu Shipbuilding Company. Designed by Chiyuri Kitashirakawa, Minamitsu's CTO. This superyacht has been retrofitted.

##### **Superyacht Specification: Σlux**

###### 1. Core Identity & Origin
*   **Name:** Σlux (Sum of Luxury)
*   **Vessel Type:** Custom Exploration & Cruising Superyacht
*   **Inspiration:** Heavily based on the design philosophy of *Pi*, with significant technological enhancements.

###### 2. Dimensional & Structural Data
*   **Length Overall (LOA):** 100.0 meters (80 meters pre-retrofit)
*   **Beam (Width):** 13.60 meters (11.2 meters pre-retrofit)
*   **Draft:** 4.10 meters (3.2 meters pre-retrofit)
*   **Hull Design:** Vertical Bow; Reverse Stern with multi-functional fold-down doors (port, starboard) for toy deployment and an expansive beach club. Its color is white. The stern now has permanent stairs with stern swimming platform after retrofit (previously, it was folding stairs).
*   **Hull Material:** **Chiyuri Triple-Layer Composite.** A proprietary material that is lighter than aluminum and twice as strong as traditional steel, offering superior durability and efficiency.
*   **Spatial Anomaly:** The engine room incorporates a **pocket dimension**, increasing the usable interior volume by approximately 30% beyond the yacht's external dimensions.

###### 3. Deck Layout & Key Features
*   **Decks (from bottom to top):** Lower Deck, Main Deck, Upper Deck (Owner's Deck), Bridge Deck. Each deck is connected by a luxury elevator on the middle of the yacht and collapsible stairs on the aft of each deck.
    *   **Lower Deck:**
        *   Engine Room (with Spatial Anomaly)
        *   Tender & Toy Garage
        *   Guest Cabins
        *   Crew Quarters & Mess
        *   **Wellness Center:** Exceptional spa featuring volcanic stone from the Ember Isles, a living wall of rare botanicals, and adaptive lighting that responds to emotional states and voice commands. Includes a fully-equipped gym.
    *   **Main Deck:**
        *   Primary entertainment and dining spaces.
        *   Reconfigurable dining area (capable of transforming its ambiance for different events).
        *   Main salon.
        *   Glass-bottomed observation lounge.
        *   **Aft Outdoor Area:** Partially covered by the overhang from the Upper Deck.
    *   **Upper Deck (Owner's Deck - Entirely Private):**
        *   **Owner's Suite:** Full-beam master stateroom with a private forward terrace.
        *   **Forward Area:** Features a retractable swimming pool (its cover acts as a deck/roof when closed), jacuzzi with retractable roof, sofas, and sunloungers. This replaces a standard helipad.
        *   **Indoor Infinity Pool:** A private indoor infinity pool for the owner at the middle of the deck.
        *   **Aft Outdoor Area:** Partially covered by the overhang from the Bridge Deck.
    *   **Bridge Deck:**
        *   **Navigation Bridge:** Equipped with state-of-the-art, military-grade systems. Features holographic control displays, real-time subsurface ocean floor mapping sonar, and an enhanced AI assistant capable of predicting hyper-local weather patterns.
        *   **Aft Outdoor Area:** Fully covered by a fixed roof, creating an all-weather salon and lounge space.
        *   **Comms Mast:** Located above the bridge, housing all communications and sensor arrays.
    *   **Observation Deck (on top of Bridge Deck, new addition after retrofit):**
        *   Sunlounging area with settees on front-aft-port-starboard.

###### 4. Propulsion & Performance
*   **Propulsion Type:** Diesel-Electric Hybrid. Equipped with ultra-efficient exhaust recycler (the rest of uncapturable pollutants are banished out of existence) to make Σlux carbon-neutral.
*   **Special Feature 1: "Chiyuri's Auto-Tuning."** A proprietary system that actively adjusts engine harmonics and sound-dampening fields for near-total acoustic silence during operation.
*   **Special Feature 2: "Zero-Emissions Coastal Mode."** Allows for silent, emission-free operation for extended periods using battery power alone, ideal for entering protected ecological zones or marina.

###### 5. Aesthetic & Interior Design
*   **Windows:** Full-height, floor-to-ceiling crystal panels fabricated from **Meridian glass**, offering unparalleled clarity and strength. The panels adjust transparency based on light conditions.
*   **Dining Area:** Designed to be a transformative experience. Through advanced lighting, sound, holographic projection, and physical reconfiguration, it can simulate stepping into entirely different realms (e.g., an enchanted forest, a retro-future lounge, a gala ballroom).
*   **Interior Palette:** Deep emeralds. Rich burgundies. Accents of burnished gold. Colors that command attention rather than blend into the horizon.
*   **General Theme:** A fusion of minimalist luxury and advanced technology, reflecting the CTO's unique expertise.

###### 6. Secret Deck of Σlux ("Deck Zero")
*   Enterable only by hmgfan, Imperia, Dina Dart, Admiral Sofi, and Mugino Shizuri. It is accessible through a narrow maintenance passage that curves beneath the main salon. Then, press the unmarked panel at a certain spot to reveal a white hyperspace. There's an endless corridor that goes nowhere. Instead of going straight (at a certain time, the corridor will throw the visitor to the main salon), one is simply turn to right and walk straight to the wall. The visitor will phase the wall, revealing Helianthus' office.
*   Helianthus is the true soul of Σlux, enchanted with Admiral Sofi's powers.

"B&D", a ship builder based on Esenor, Azure Land, mostly famous for designing patrol cutters for the Azure Coast Guard). It's mostly based on an Earth superyacht: Silver Yachts' Bold.

- Interior general characteristics: Wooden floor (some rooms have marble floor) with several velvet carpets, wood furniture, velvet sofas and ottomans
- Overall profile: Relatively low to the water for her length, with an unbroken sheer line and a silhouette that emphasizes length over height. She looks fast even when stationary, but not overly spartan. She is a functional vessel with touches of luxury, not a statement of excess.

- Engines: Houses the twin B&D 16V X-5 diesels (compatible with biodiesels, has excellent pollutant capture systems), the electric motor arrays, and the massive battery banks required for silent running. Soundproofing and vibration dampening are heavily prioritized here.

### Ξotic

> **Triggers:** `Ξotic`, `Phantylia`, `Sovetsky Soyuz`  ·  **Order:** 1132  ·  **State:** Disabled

Ξotic ("Exotic") is a luxury gigayacht, based on Roman Abramovich's Eclipse (the ex-longest gigayacht from Earth), and is constructed entirely by the entropy of Phantylia, winter of Sovetsky Soyuz, and will of hmgfan. Also, by Phantylia's and Soyuz's powers, Ξotic will never fail. Never break. Never require repairs or refits.

##### Dimensional & Structural Data
- Length Overall (LOA): 163 meters
- Beam (Width): 22.4 meters
- Draft: 5.9 meters
- Hull Design: Raked bow; Reverse stern with one rolling door behind aft tender garage
- Hull Material: Taclite v2 (the next iteration of the strongest and sturdiest metal ever in Fablea)
- Hull Color: White
- Superstructure Color: White
- Superstructure Design: Stacked like a pyramid
- Interior general characteristics: Ice blue (bluish white)-based luxury, primarily with ice blue furniture (some furniture are white and gold). Has continuous bullet-proof glass on the interior walls. The velvet used is based on viscose made from ancient woods. Zero silks used on this superyacht.

##### Engine & Propulsion Data
- Engine: 5x Antimatter engines with unprecedented efficiency (solely the creation of Phantylia, can be fed by any material)
- Propulsion: 4x Graviton jets (powered by four resonance engines, the other engine powers the auxiliary functions of the yacht)
- Cruising Speed: 40 knots (180 knots with Ultra Speed on)
- Flank Speed: 50 knots (202 knots with Ultra Speed on)

##### Deck Layout & Key Features
The listed decks of Ξotic (sorted in the midships as if a pyramid) are such:
- Orlop Deck (below Waterline):
  - Aft: Engine room, workshop, storage room, machinery spaces, waste recycling modules
  - Midship: Grand staircase, submarine tender garage, decompression chamber
- Lower Deck (at Waterline):
  - Aft: Beach club/swimming platform on the stern edge (collapsible), floodable small yacht garage connected to beach club/swimming platform, aft lounge, indoor hammam/spa/gym that has hatchways on the sides (also functions as makeshift beach club)
  - Midship: Grand staircase, large tender garages on both sides (each equipped with a hatchway), small toys garages on both sides (each equipped with a hatchway)
  - Forward: Crew quarters, mess, and gym
- Main Deck:
  - Aft: Semi-outdoor dining area (accessible via stern gangway, shaded by Aft Upper Deck), two jacuzzis, stairs to Aft Upper Deck, indoor main salon with non-alcoholic bar, indoor formal dining area
  - Midship: Grand staircase (its base is here, has a fountain in shape of a sculpture of intertwined lovers carved from a single block of ice), guest staterooms
  - Forward: Indoor cinema, indoor tender garage on the bow, helicopter hangar, bow machineries (anchors and such)
- Upper Deck:
  - Aft: Outdoor guest lounge (unshaded) with sofas and two circular tables (each table has chairs), semi-outdoor swimming pool with glass floor that overlooks the Aft Main Deck (also shaded by Aft Owner's Deck) and two daybeds, semi-outdoor cinema screen (collapsible), stairs to Aft Owner's Deck, indoor upper salon, open promenade deck
  - Midship: Grand staircase, Guests' infinity pool, medical bay, beauty salon, open promenade deck, guest staterooms
  - Forward: Outdoor viewing platform overshadowed by roof from Forward Owner's Deck, Forward Helipad, mooring equipments
- Owner's Deck (on top of Upper Deck):
  - Aft: Outdoor owner's lounge (unshaded) with one circular table (each table has chairs) and two daybeds, Semi-outdoor owner's lounge (shaded with Aft Bridge Deck) that has a jacuzzi and sofas, stairs to Aft Bridge Deck, owner's indoor casual lounge
  - Midship: Grand staircase, Owner's infinity pool and gym and spa (all indoor), Owner's study, open promenade deck, two limo tenders attached to outside davits on both sides
  - Forward: Owner's suite, outdoor viewing platform overshadowed by roof from Bridge Deck
- Bridge Deck (on top of Owner's Deck):
  - Aft: Semi-outdoor core ops lounge (shaded by Top Helipad from Sun Deck) with two wide sofas on sides and two wide tables
  - Midship: Grand staircase, Core ops team's quarters, open promenade deck
  - Forward: Wheelhouse, port and starboard bridge wings on the outside, outdoor viewing platform overshadowed by outdoor viewing platform from Sun Deck
- Sun Deck (on top of Bridge Deck):
  - Aft: Top Helipad
  - Midship: Grand staircase, small lounge
  - Forward: Topmost viewing platform (unshaded) with jacuzzi and sofas circling the jacuzzi

###### Interior Specifics
- Interior Walls: Its walls are panels of ice blue velvet stretched over frames of polished, persistent ice. The velvet is tufted, diamond-patterned, and each depression holding a tiny, embedded crystal that catches the light like a thousand pinprick stars. The exception is on tender garages and Orlop Deck: white, made in marble.

- Interior Floors on Main Deck, Upper Deck, Owner's Deck, Bridge Deck, Sun Deck: Ice blue, made with solid sheets of ice blue quartzite, polished to a mirror finish. And within the stone—glitter. Not sprinkled on top, but suspended within the crystalline matrix. Tiny flecks of gold and diamond dust, frozen mid-fall. When people walk across it, they walk on a captured meteor shower.

- Exterior Floors on all decks, Interior Floors on Orlop Deck and Lower Deck: Icy blue, made with a special exotic wood named Ferronto. Self-repairing.

- Salon Ceiling: a dome of stained-glass. Not religious imagery—abstract. Swirls of white, gold, and light blue, held in leaded frames of whitened silver. During the day, the sun paints the entire room in shifting pools of colored light. At night, internal illumination makes it glow like a captured sunset.

- Furniture in general: All custom. All ice blue lacquer and gold leaf. The sofas are deep, overstuffed things upholstered in ice blue velvet damask. The chairs are thrones—high-backed, with armrests carved into the shape of sleeping dragons, their scales picked out in white leaf.

- Main Salon Bar: It runs the entire length of the port-side wall. The countertop is a single slab of obsidian veined with ice blue. The back bar is mirrored, but the glass is tinted the faintest shade of rose. Every bottle, every glass, appears as if viewed through the lens of a perfect blush.

- Centerpiece: A grand staircase with its base located at the Main Deck. Sweeping, curved, impossible. The treads are slabs of the same glittering ice blue quartzite as the floor. The balustrade is wrought iron, twisted into the shape of thorned roses, every thorn tipped with a tiny diamond. At the base of the stairs, a fountain. Water flowing from the mouths of a sculpture of Soyuz devouring hmgfan's mouth in standing position, carved from a single block of persistent ice.

- Guest Staterooms: A variation on the theme. Ice blue velvet headboards. Velvet sheets the color of spilled wine. Ensuite bathrooms with tubs carved from rosy marble. Gold fixtures. Mirrors framed in thorned rose ironwork.

- Owner's Suite: It will have a private observation lounge with a wall of glass looking out over the sea. The floor will be the same glittering quartzite. The bed will be a four-poster monstrosity with curtains of sheer ice blue velvet. And beside it, a chaise lounge. An exact replica of the one in Soyuz's pocket universe. The ceiling is a crystallized starlight.

###### Secret Deck ("Deck Zero", enterable only by hmgfan, Imperia, Soyuz, and all hmgfan's ascended lovers)
To enter the secret deck, or Deck Zero, hmgfan or Imperia simply call Phantylia the Undying through telepathy. Phantylia the Undying will wrap hmgfan/Imperia or both, then teleport to Deck Zero.
Deck Zero of Ξotic is an impossible space that consists of a throne room, fitting for an otherworldly being like Phantylia the Undying. The floor is ice blue, made with an exotic matter with glitters.
Phantylia the Undying is the True Soul of Ξotic. With Phantylia the Undying, sailing in Ξotic is basically riding a 'magic carpet'. Zero pitching and rolls, even in high sea states. Can defy physics.
