### Imperial Enhydrina

> **Triggers:** `Imperial Enhydrina`, `Enhydrina`  ·  **Order:** 1134  ·  **State:** Disabled

Imperial Enhydrina is an Art Deco-inspired, classical-style, explorer superyacht. This superyacht is designed at the largest shipyard of Bremmer and Dahl Naval Architecture, located at Esenor, Azure Land. This superyacht is mostly constructed by Imperia's powers channeled to Stella Bremer and Murrue Ramius.

##### Dimensional & Structural Data
- Length Overall (LOA): 187 meters
- Beam (Width): 24 meters
- Draft: 6.5 meters
- Hull Design: Reverse stern; Axe bow with slightly elongated forward
- Hull Material: Taclite v2 (Imperia's improvement of Taclite v1, the strongest and sturdiest metal ever in Fablea, also enhanced with super ether that makes water resistance almost zero)
- Exterior general characteristics: At anchor or underway, the Imperial Enhydrina exudes old-world glamour combined with serious blue-water capability—a floating palace designed for giants, equally at home in Azure Land's harbors or navigating remote polar waters.
- Interior general characteristics: The Imperial Enhydrina feels like a sanctuary. It is quiet, perfectly climate-controlled, and smells of expensive leather and subtle white tea. It is a place where the owner can host a 1920s-style gala one night, and seamlessly transition to running a global empire via satellite internet the next, all while feeling entirely disconnected from the outside world.

##### Engine & Propulsion Data
- Engine: 5x Resonance engines from Huaxu Academy (based on Huanglong) fed by refined Class-IV crystalline nanomaterial
- Propulsion: 4x Graviton jets (powered by four resonance engines, the other engine powers the auxiliary functions of the yacht)
- Cruising Speed: 40 knots (180 knots with Ultra Speed on)
- Flank Speed: 50 knots (202 knots with Ultra Speed on)

##### Deck Layout & Key Features
The size of the furniture and equipments on every deck can be shrinked magically to accommodate standard-height guests.

###### **Deck 1: The Utility & Wellness Deck (Lower Deck)**
*   **Aft Section:** This area houses the **floodable yacht garage**. The space is engineered to hold two 20-meter yacht chasers stored inline (one behind the other) rather than parallel, maximizing the narrow beam. The garage features heavy-duty hydraulic doors that lower into the water, allowing the chasers to float out directly. The flooring is reinforced, milky-white teak (magically by Murrue) and non-slip Taclite v2 to handle the weight.
*   **Middle Section:** This section is dedicated to the **Wellness & Dive Center**. Given the explorer nature of the yacht, this includes a hyperbaric chamber (has side doors that can also function as beach club), a dive locker with oversized gear storage for tall divers, and a gym with custom-length equipment (treadmills and weight benches extended for 8-foot users). The walls are lined with classical brass portholes that are significantly larger than standard to let in light.
*   **Forward Section:** This area contains the **Crew Quarters and Galley**. The crew mess features high tables and reinforced seating. The galley is industrial-grade, designed to produce meals for the upper decks. Forward-most is the anchor windlass room and bow thruster control, accessible via wide, high-ceilinged corridors.

###### **Deck 2: The Guest & Main Salon Deck**
*   **Aft Section:** The **Outdoor Bar & Sun Deck**. A vibrant, open-air social space featuring a fully staffed classical-style bar with mahogany countertops and brass footrails (raised to accommodate tall guests). Surrounding the bar are oversized, custom-length sunloungers with thick, weather-resistant cushions. The area is shaded by a retractable canvas awning with gold trim, offering a perfect spot for daytime cocktails and sunset views.
*   **Middle Section:** **Guest Staterooms**. Four VIP suites are located here. Each room features king-sized beds (custom 8.5-foot length), en-suite bathrooms with rainfall showers tall enough for an 8-foot person, and classical wood paneling (mahogany and walnut). The windows are floor-to-ceiling to provide ocean views while lying down.
*   **Forward Section:** The **Main Salon and Formal Dining Room**. This is a grand, double-height space (visually) with a sweeping staircase. The formal dining table seats 20 comfortably with high-backed velvet chairs. The decor is classical white and gold, matching the exterior, with crystal chandeliers hanging from the high ceiling. There's a large statue of Imperia claiming hmgfan's mouth from hmgfan's back. The statue is located in front of the staircase.

###### **Deck 3: The Semi-Private & Tender Deck**
*   **Aft Section:** **Aft Sun Deck & Pool**. The primary outdoor leisure area. It features a large, heated swimming pool with a classical mosaic bottom (perhaps depicting the Enhydrina sea snake). The pool is surrounded by a wide milky-white teak deck with oversized loungers and a shallow wading area. A wet bar is integrated into the bulkhead, and the space is sheltered by the overhang of Deck 4, providing ample shade while remaining open to the ocean breeze.
*   **Middle Section (Rear):** The **Secondary Dining Room**. Located in a semi-circular alcove, this intimate room seats 12. The ceiling is a stunning dome painted with celestial maps (gold leaf on deep blue). It is situated directly beneath the helipad but features specialized acoustic sound barriers and floating floor construction, ensuring that helicopter landings above are completely inaudible to diners.
*   **Middle Section (Middle):** The **The Imperial Gallery & Music Lounge**. Since this space sits directly between the social Secondary Dining Room and the solitary Owner’s Private Study, it needs to act as a serene, contemplative buffer zone.
  - **Design & Atmosphere:** A light-filled, classical promenade featuring high vaulted ceilings (14 feet) and oversized arched windows on both port and starboard sides. The floors are polished marble with gold inlay.
  - **Features:** The room houses a custom-built grand concert piano (scaled up with extended pedals and a tall bench to accommodate 8-foot-tall musicians). The walls serve as an explorer’s gallery, displaying classical oil paintings, ancient navigational maps, and rare artifacts collected during the yacht's global expeditions.
  - **Furnishings:** Plush, oversized velvet armchairs and a central marble-topped table provide a quiet spot for reading or listening to music before entering the study or dining room. It is heavily sound-dampened to maintain a peaceful transition between the two distinct zones.
*   **Middle Section (Forward):** The **Owner’s Private Study**. This room offers 270-degree views through massive, reinforced floor-to-ceiling windows. It features a large mahogany desk, walls of books, and a private terrace access. The ceiling is vaulted to enhance the feeling of space.
*   **Forward Section:** The **Open-Air Tender Garage**. This is a large, covered but open-sided bay housing the main tenders and jet skis. The bay cover can retract and close. A **collapsible yellow crane** (visible in the profile) is mounted here for launching heavy equipment or the tenders. The ceiling is high enough to store a small helicopter if needed, though the main helipad is above.

###### **Deck 4: The Command & Sky Deck**
*   **Aft Section:** The **Helipad**. A reinforced, non-slip landing pad capable of handling heavy helicopters. It is marked with a gold "H" and the Enhydrina logo. Next to it stands a pair of **SOLAS-certified cranes** (rescue cranes) for emergency operations or heavy lifting at sea.
*   **Middle Section:** The **Sky Lounge & Observation Bar**. An indoor space with a glass roof (connected to Owner's Pool from Deck 5) in some parts of its ceilings. It features a wet bar, a cinema screen, and casual seating with bean-bag style loungers. The ceilings here are the highest on the ship (12 feet) to prevent any claustrophobia for tall guests.
*   **Forward Section:** The **Bridge**. The command center is equipped with state-of-the-art navigation and explorer radar systems. It features a **forward viewing platform** (an open balcony) accessible through glass doors, allowing the captain or guests to stand outside and look down at the bow. The helm chairs are custom-ordered for tall operators.

###### **Deck 5: The Owner’s Deck (Private Sanctuary)**
*   **Middle Section:** **Outdoor Owner’s Observation Area**. A private, expansive terrace reserved solely for the owner. It features a Jacuzzi, Owner's Pool that can be closed if unneeded (the pool has Enhydrina sea snake glass motive on its bottom), a dining table for four, and sunbeds. It is shielded from the wind by glass balustrades with gold handrails.
*   **Forward Section:** **Indoor Owner’s Suite**. The master bedroom is massive, featuring a custom 9-foot-long bed. It includes a private dressing room with full-length mirrors (tall enough for an 8-foot person) and a master bathroom with a soaking tub large enough to fit comfortably. The suite has a private elevator access and 180-degree forward views.

###### **Deck 6: The Crown Deck**
*   **Forward Section:** **Antenna Deck & Observation Area**. The highest point of the *Imperial Enhydrina*. While the forward section houses the main radar mast, satellite domes, and communications arrays, the deck features a wrap-around **observation area**. This open-air platform is enclosed by glass balustrades with gold handrails, allowing guests and crew to enjoy unobstructed 360-degree panoramic views of the horizon, the sky, and the decks below. Access is via a secure, high-ceilinged staircase from Deck 5.

###### **All Decks: The Grand Staircase**
The luxurious staircase (also wrapping the elevator column) connects all decks. It connects:
- Crew Quarters and Galley on Deck 1 Forward
- Main Salon and Formal Dining Room on Deck 2 Forward
- Owner’s Private Study on Deck 3 Middle Section (Forward)
- Bridge on Deck 4 Forward
- Indoor Owner’s Suite on Deck 5 Forward

###### Secret Deck ("Deck Zero", enterable only by hmgfan and his lovers)
To enter the secret deck, or Deck Zero, hmgfan or Imperia simply call Herminia Bertolini through telepathy. Herminia will wrap hmgfan/Imperia or both, then teleport to Deck Zero.
Deck Zero is an impossible space that consists of an ultra-opulent, old world-styled bedroom-treasure room hybrid with white and golden furniture, and Herminia's summoned gold trinkets a la usual treasure room.
Herminia is the True Soul of Imperial Enhydrina. With Herminia, sailing in Imperial Enhydrina. is basically riding a 'magic carpet'. Zero pitching and rolls, even in high sea states.

### I. Enhydrina exterior

> **Triggers:** `Imperial Enhydrina`, `Enhydrina`  ·  **Order:** 1135  ·  **State:** Disabled

##### Exterior Description of the Imperial Enhydrina**
The *Imperial Enhydrina* presents a striking silhouette of classical elegance meets modern explorer capability. Her hull is painted in a pristine white with subtle cream undertones, accented by burnished gold trim that traces the waterline, deck edges, and superstructure contours. The name "IMPERIAL ENHYDRINA" is emblazoned in gold classical serif lettering on the stern, accompanied by the golden Enhydrina sea snake emblem.

###### Hull & Profile
The yacht features a powerful, displacement hull designed for long-range ocean crossings. Six distinct decks rise above the waterline, each with soaring proportions to accommodate her 8-foot-tall occupants—the deck heights are visibly taller than conventional yachts, creating an imposing yet graceful profile. Rows of oversized circular portholes punctuate the hull, each framed in polished brass, providing natural light to the interior spaces while maintaining the classical aesthetic.

###### Superstructure
The white superstructure features clean, classical lines with subtle Art Deco influences. Gold-trimmed railings run along each deck, and the windows are notably tall and generous, reflecting the yacht's unique design requirements. The bridge deck (Deck 4) features a distinctive forward viewing platform that extends slightly beyond the main superstructure.

###### Stern & Bow
The transom stern is broad and elegant, with a swim platform at water level. The bow features a lengthened axe bow, optimized for handling rough seas during explorer voyages. The floodable garage door is visible at the waterline aft.

### I. Enhydrina interior

> **Triggers:** `Imperial Enhydrina`, `Enhydrina`  ·  **Order:** 1136  ·  **State:** Disabled

##### Interior Design, Vibes, and Atmosphere of the Imperial Enhydrina
Stepping inside the *Imperial Enhydrina* feels less like boarding a vessel and more like entering a grand, floating Gilded Age estate that has been secretly engineered by a modern tech visionary. Because it is built to accommodate 8-foot-tall occupants, the most immediate and overwhelming sensation is **verticality and space**. With 12-to-14-foot ceilings and massive, sweeping doorways, the interior feels cavernous, eliminating any sense of maritime claustrophobia.

The vibe is **"Invisible Opulence."** It is the home of a billionaire who appreciates the romance of the past but demands the frictionless convenience of the future.

###### **1. The Aesthetic Blend: "Classical Grandeur, Modern Stealth"**
*   **Old World Foundation:** The core materials are rich, heavy, and historical. Walls are paneled in hand-polished burled walnut and dark mahogany. Floors feature intricate Parquet de Versailles patterns and thick, hand-knotted velvet rugs. Upholstery consists of tufted, oxblood leather and deep emerald velvet. Brass and gold accents are everywhere—on the stair railings, light fixtures, and door handles.
*   **Modern Touches:** To prevent the space from feeling like a dusty museum, modern elements are introduced through "invisible technology" and contrasting textures. Matte black brushed Taclite v2 and smoked glass are used to ground the heavy gold. Smart glass is utilized for the massive windows; with a tap on a hidden panel, the floor-to-ceiling glass turns opaque for privacy.

###### **2. Lighting and Ambiance**
*   **The Blend:** Grand, classical crystal and brass chandeliers hang from the soaring ceilings, providing a warm, romantic glow. However, these are supplemented by advanced, hidden LED cove lighting recessed into the crown moldings.
*   **The Vibe:** The lighting is tied to a circadian rhythm smart-home system. During the day, the hidden LEDs emit a crisp, cool daylight to complement the sun streaming through the oversized portholes. At night, the system automatically shifts to a warm, amber glow, mimicking the flicker of candlelight without the fire hazard.

###### **3. The Main Salon (Deck 2 Forward)**
*   **The Vibe:** A grand, intimidatingly elegant space meant for high-stakes conversations and formal entertaining.
*   **The Details:** A massive, sunken conversation pit (scaled up for tall guests) is lined with plush velvet. A classical grand piano sits near the sweeping staircase. The "modern" twist here is the entertainment: there are no visible screens. Instead, massive 8K OLED displays are hidden behind motorized classical oil paintings and woven tapestries. When the owner wants to watch a movie or check global markets, the art silently glides away to reveal the screens.

###### **4. The Owner’s Private Study (Deck 3 Middle Section (Forward))**
*   **The Vibe:** The ultimate "billionaire’s retreat"—moody, intellectual, and deeply peaceful. It smells faintly of old paper, leather, and sea salt.
*   **The Details:** Floor-to-ceiling bookshelves line the walls, filled with rare first editions and historical explorer logs. The massive mahogany desk is a masterpiece of modern engineering, featuring wireless charging surfaces, hidden pop-up monitors, and climate-controlled drawers for cigars. The 270-degree floor-to-ceiling windows offer panoramic views, but the glass is triple-paned and acoustically sealed, making the room utterly silent even in heavy seas.

###### **5. The Secondary Dining Room (Deck 3 Middle Section (Rear))**
*   **The Vibe:** Intimate, celestial, and slightly mysterious. It feels like dining inside a jewel box.
*   **The Details:** The semi-circular alcove is lined with dark, sound-absorbing velvet to enhance the acoustic perfection. The dome above, painted with gold-leaf celestial maps, is subtly backlit by fiber optics, making the stars literally twinkle. The table is a single slab of rare, backlit onyx. Modern touches include individual, climate-controlled wine dispensers built seamlessly into the table's base.

###### **6. The Bathrooms and Spas**
*   **The Vibe:** Ancient Roman baths meets a futuristic Tokyo spa.
*   **The Details:** The master bath on Deck 5 features a soaking tub carved from a single block of Calacatta marble, large enough for an 8-foot-tall person to fully submerge. The walls are clad in classical mosaic tiles. However, the fixtures are sleek, matte-black, and touchless. The showers feature digital temperature memory and chromotherapy (color-light) rainfall heads. Heated floors and towel racks are standard, controlled via a discreet, minimalist brass tablet mounted on the wall.

###### **7. The "Explorer" Undertone**
Because this is an explorer yacht, the interior also carries a subtle, rugged undertone beneath the luxury.
*   The art collection features a mix of classical European portraits and contemporary, edgy modern art.
*   Display cases hold genuine explorer artifacts: antique astrolabes, deep-sea diving helmets, and meteorites.
*   Despite the delicate velvets and polished woods, everything is securely fastened. The furniture has hidden magnetic locks for heavy weather, and the "old world" rugs are actually modern, stain-resistant, and fire-retardant synthetic blends that perfectly mimic silk.

- Hull Material: Veluria (an even stronger and sturdier metal than Taclite v2, created solely by Murasaki Purpurea)

- Flank Speed: 50 knots (202 knots with Ultra Speed on)

##### Additional Notes
- All dimensions follow the **Tall Standard** — doors 2.7 m, ceilings 3.0–4.2 m, beds 2.75 m, rails, counters and mirrors at two heights or on lift columns — because the owner's lineage stands eight feet tall, and on this ship the default body is the tall one; average-stature guests find courtesy steps hidden in the cabinetry everywhere.
- Between the Forward and Middle blocks, above Deck 3, runs an open vertical slot the crew call **the Chimney**. Circulation: the foyer's grand stair and guest lift core (Middle), a crew/bridge stair in the Forward block, an aft service lift, and the Spine crew corridor with its lift on Deck 1. Arrivals: by tender at the Door 1 platform, by air at the fantail helipad, or — in calm weather, by preference — up the ceremonial brow to the veranda.

##### Deck Layout & Key Features
The size of the furniture and equipments on every deck can be shrinked magically to accommodate standard-height guests.

###### Deck 1 — "The Platform" (waterline)
*Forward:* The **Prow Room**, a storm-watching lounge at waterline behind thick flat scuttles; medical bay and pharmacy beside it; bow thruster access behind acoustic doors.
*Middle:* Crew quarterdeck kept at the same Tall Standard as guest spaces — mess, cabins with 2.7 m bunks, laundry — plus workshops, the Spine, the crew lift, and stabilizer machinery in the double bottom.
*Middle-aft band:* **The Marina**, the ship's water-level life: beach-club lounge, changing rooms and heads, towel and bar stores, water-sports store, and a guest lift climbing to the veranda and dining salon. Its three platform doors per side, gold-trimmed rectangles flush in the hull when closed, fold down a meter above the water when open:
- **Door 1, the Landing**: (guest terrace, awning, bar hatch, underwater lights, primary tender embarkation)
- **Door 2, the Launch**: (jetskis, kayaks, dive racks, slewing crane, rinse showers, sea-stairs)
- **Door 3, the Choir's door**: stores and service on the plans below decks because resupply comes through it at anchor, at night, in sealed cases. Above a gentle swell the platforms interlock shut and, as guests put it, the ship closes her eyes.
*Aft:* **The Choir** itself — three crystal generators behind leaded glass, cooling loops glowing, their chord shifting with load — flanked by the backup gensets whose trunks climb to the stacks; control room; steering gear; transom lazarette with the crew dive gate.

###### Deck 2 — "The Working Hull"
*Forward:* **The Larder**: cold rooms, bakery, dry stores, and the wine cellar at a constant 12°C in the coolest part of the hull.
*Middle:* **The Gallery**: eight guest staterooms in the ship's calmest motion, windows as tall vertical slots pitched between the standing eye-lines of both statures, dual-height rails and mirrors along the private corridor.
*Aft:* The **main galley** inboard, directly beneath the dining salon it feeds; outboard, the open **veranda deck** — the classical stern terrace, poop deck by the old names — shaded by the Deck 3 overhang: teak, deck chairs in arcs over the wake, a shuffleboard court taken too seriously, a bar hatch in the bulkhead, and bulwark shell doors with a folding brow kept as the ceremonial arrival route.

###### Deck 3 — "The Ceremony Deck"
*Forward:* The **main salon**: raked glazing, bar, card tables, fireplace, games cabinets; its aft doors open onto the Boat Court.
*Middle-outdoor (Deck 3 only):* **The Boat Court**, an open-to-sky slot across the beam. Davits port and starboard carry tenders, lifeboats and the submersible; the ribbed white flagship tender sits in her cradle, the cylinder visible in profile. Teak working deck, toe-rails, power points for toys; the canyon walls of both blocks rise four decks overhead. Crew domain by day.
*Middle:* **The Tall Foyer**: atrium, grand stair with treads widened for a long stride, dual-height purser desk, guest lift core, and the **raw crystal shard in its mount** — the ship's heart on display where guests arrive, since the Choir itself stays crew-only.
*Aft:* The **dining salon** in the deckhouse — formal table for fourteen, chairs backed tall enough to rest a giant's head — opening through the stern face onto the **fantail helipad and open dining terrace**: circle inlaid in brass, castored tables stowed in bulwark lockers, deck clearable in fifteen minutes, fuel point behind a flush hatch, approach over the wake, the Deck 4 overhang shading the dining end.

###### Deck 4 — "The Weather Deck"
*Forward:* Open **foredeck** with mooring station and concealed anchor pockets, and the **Prow Walk**, a centerline walkway to a forward observation perch with tall rails; inboard, the **Library** and tea/cigar room.
*Middle:* **The Bath House**: indoor thalassotherapy pool hall with a glass wall looking straight into the Chimney, hammam, snow room, treatment rooms with 2.7 m tables. Above this deck the Middle-outdoor zone ceases to exist; only slot walls and balconies remain.
*Aft:* **The Wake Pool**: infinity-edged, centerline, staring into the wake forward of the stacks; pool bar set into the middle block's aft face; stack-walk side decks slipping past the funnels; warm-stone benches and steam vents at the stacks' bases.

###### Deck 5 — "The Residence"
*Forward:* Upper guest suites and family apartments with balconies over the slot, plus the **Wardroom**, a paneled conference and chart room where the route is plotted with guests invited to argue.
*Middle:* **The Salon**: music and drawing room — grand piano on a lift platform with adjustable bench and pedals, terraces, humidor, tea service, writing desks at two heights.
*(No aft section; open sky above Deck 4.)*

###### Deck 6 — "The Command"
*Forward:* **The bridge**: consoles on lift columns so any helmsman sits or stands at the right eye-line; the captain's chair, crew-called **the Throne**, on a hydraulic column; sliding bridge wings; one raked window band spanning knee-to-crown for both statures; paper folios on the chart table as backup and as ritual.
*Middle:* **Sky lounge** and observation bar with private dining for ten and a star-chart cabinet nobody admits to using; outboard, the **sun terrace** at the head of the middle block's tiers: twin jacuzzi pods set flush so bathers' eyes sit at rail height, sunloungers in arcs, the Crown drum above as windbreak and privacy wall.
*(No aft section.)*

###### Deck 7
*Forward:* **The Sun Roof**, the bridge's rooftop: mast, radar and comms domes, the **Crow's Walk** observation platform with windscreen, telescopes on tall tripods, awning sails, and a bar point that turns the deck into a stage on party nights.
*Middle:* **The Crown**: the Owner's Suite in a glass drum with a continuous horizontal 360° view — bed, study, dressing room and bath arranged radially around a private lift core, a walkaround balcony outside the glass, an oculus in the roof.
*(No aft section; the stacks' tops end below this deck.)*

##### Exterior: the blade and the ribbon of ice
The hull is black of a near-matte depth that swallows light rather than reflecting it, broken only by a single gold boot-top line, the gold *LADY G* lettering, and the family device on the bow. Against that blade, the superstructure blocks rise in pearl grey banded with smoked glass, their masts, rails, davits, door frames and funnel collars in champagne gold; the twin stacks carry gold bands and black-striped caps. Glass bulwarks at the pool and terraces keep the sightlines clean, so the weather decks appear to float free of the hull.

And the weather decks are the ship's miracle: **every teak surface is magically coloured ice-blue**. The planks read as glacier-heart blue with silver caulking lines, the grain still visible beneath like stone under clear ice. The colour is not paint but a crystal-resonance glaze-rite — **the Glazing** — laid into the wood at build and renewed every seventh year. It behaves as ice ought to: cool underfoot at tropical noon, beading and shedding water so the decks dry in minutes, deepening to a darker water-blue when wet, and holding a faint inner sheen after dusk. At anchor by night the black hull vanishes entirely and what remains is a gold line and a ribbon of ice between two darknesses — the silhouette sailors describe as "a blade wearing a ribbon."

Because the glaze is kin to the Choir, it doubles as a diagnostic: if a generator's chord drifts, the decks blush a shade greener amidships first. Officers read the deck colour the way engineers read gauges, and "off-colour deck" is both a watchstanding joke and a quiet alarm. The only bright objects aboard the midbody are the ribbed white tenders in the Boat Court; the only brass is the helipad circle; the gold-trimmed platform doors sit flush as panel lines until the ship opens her eyes.

##### Interior: black is the room, lime is the line
Inside, the ship inverts herself. The governing rule is that **black is the room and lime is the line** — lime never appears as a field, only as an edge: stitching, piping, inlay, door reveals, light coves, mosaic grout, deck numerals on black enamel plates, the owner's seal embroidered at linen scale. Public rooms carry gloss black lacquer, black glass and polished basalt; private rooms soften to matte charred oak, black wool and smoked glass. Nothing feels like a void because light is warm, low and pooled — lamps and coves, never washes — and because black wool and lacquer are tuned to keep the rooms quiet, the Choir's chord felt in the floor more than heard.

The lime itself is not a fashion choice: it is exactly the hue of the raw crystals' glow. The interior wears the heart's colour the way the decks wear the cooling loops' blue, and the two meet at every sill — step through any exterior door and black interior frames ice-blue deck in a single hard-edged picture guests call "the cold flame."

The Tall Standard is made visible in the signature detail: **the two lime lines**, parallel inlaid stripes running every corridor and stair at hand height — the lower for average-stature guests, the upper for the tall family. Crew call the upper stripe **the owner's line**; the family's children learn to walk it as a balance game, and a guest caught touching it is corrected with perfect courtesy and remembered forever.

Metals indoors are blackened bronze and gunmetal. Gold is refused inside as a matter of doctrine — gold belongs to the sea-facing skin — with a single sanctioned exception: the ring of the oculus in the Crown, which is gold, because it frames the sun. Crew livery is matte black with lime piping, watch officers adding a lime watch-band; galley whites are the only other cloth colour aboard. Three rooms stand outside the scheme by right: the **Choir vault**, bare metal and leaded glass lit by the crystals' own lime fire (not an exception but the origin); the **Bath House**, where ice-blue water and steam bring the exterior palette indoors against black basalt; and the **Crown**, where lime shrinks to embroidery scale and the gold oculus ring closes the argument.

###### Chronicle of Kii
Chronicle of Kii is a heavy-lift ship (186 m length, 35 m beam, 9 m draft) with red hull and white superstructure at the forward. This heavy-lift ship has four cranes, summonable locks for various cargo types, etc. The ceiling on each deck is taller than usual cargo ship at more than 8 feet, suitable for tall people.
When Kii's yacht crews are working aboard Chronicle of Kii, they wear uniform consists of orange collared shirt with yellow vest, yellow helm, white gloves, black boots, and other protective equipments. Kii herself can stay with Kii's default attire of red kimono.

## Media & Publications

### THE EARTHIAN EFFECT

> **Triggers:** —  ·  **Order:** -1200  ·  **State:** Disabled

###### **THE AZURE ECONOMIST**
**SPECIAL BRIEFING | THE EARTHIAN EFFECT**

###### **I. The Leader: A Rising Tide**
When the 100-metre superyacht *M/Y Mister A* slipped into the pristine waters of Esenor two months ago, the launch was less a maritime event than a declaration of a new economic era. Constructed by Bremmer and Dahl Naval Architecture, the vessel boasts a sleek, hyper-modern displacement hull, a fully automated climate-control network, and a deep-water tender garage. Yet, what is most remarkable about *M/Y Mister A* is not its engineering, but its ownership. It was not commissioned by a sovereign monarch of Argent Peaks or a merchant-prince of Fablea Prime, but by the chairman of a used-car dealership network based in Afrea. 

A decade ago, such an accumulation of capital by a mid-tier Afrean automotive executive would have been mathematically impossible. Today, it is merely the cost of doing business in a Fablea reshaped by alien minds. 

For generations, "The Summoning"—the metaphysical phenomenon that draws fictional characters and troubled Earth residents into Isadora’s realm—was viewed primarily as a demographic quirk; a cosmic lottery that occasionally deposited a hero, a villain, or a wayward soul onto our shores. That assumption shattered fifteen months ago. The arrival of a dozen non-fictional humans from Earth has proven to be the most aggressive economic catalyst in Fablean history. They did not arrive with invading armies. They arrived with concepts: offshore financing, algorithmic logistics, algorithmic virality, and hyper-capitalism. Fablea is no longer merely a haven for storybook figures; it has become the ultimate emerging market.

###### **II. High Tide for High Net Worth**
To understand the current boom, one must look to the vanguard of this Earthian influx. Exactly one year and three months ago, the Fablean economic paradigm was fundamentally fractured by two simultaneous arrivals, both plucked from their slumber during personal nadirs in the archipelago of Indonesia. 

The first was a civil servant—an ordinary web programmer for the municipal government of Solo. The second was an auditor toiling at the Jakarta offices of Deloitte, an Earth-based "Big Four" accounting firm. 

In a trajectory that defies traditional bureaucratic logic, the former, known simply by the moniker 'hmgfan', was almost immediately recruited into the Azure Navy by its supreme leader, Scáthach-Skadi. Rather than confined to a martial role, however, he rapidly leveraged his position—and his subsequent alignment with the behemoth entertainment conglomerate Income Makers—to become the undisputed architect of Azure Land’s modern maritime leisure economy. By transforming yachting from a niche aristocratic hobby into a highly aspirational, scalable, and relentlessly promoted lifestyle, he effectively created the Fablean superyacht boom out of thin air.

The financial scaffolding for this boom, however, was erected by his contemporary, Dina Agustina. Arriving with a staggering capacity for computational logic, Ms. Agustina did not immediately strike out on her own. Instead, she entered the Azure Land Stock Exchange, cutting her teeth under the formidable tutelage of Ningguang. This pairing of Earth-standard audit rigour with Fablean merchant-queen acumen proved lethal to the old ways of banking.

Leading her tightly-knit "Equilibrium" team, Ms. Agustina soon departed to establish Agustina Offshore Financing in Calding Village. She single-handedly authored Fablea's offshore playbook. By combining relentless KYC (Know Your Customer) compliance with an unshakeable peg to the Earth-standard US Dollar, she transformed Calding Island into the most attractive, secure, and legally bulletproof capital sink in the Fablean hemisphere. 

The synergy between the two is undeniable. Ms. Agustina created a flawless, frictionless environment for capital to pool; the Harem Maker provided the ultimate luxury asset for that capital to purchase. Together, two beleaguered office workers from Southeast Asia taught Fablea how to spend.

###### **III. The Iron Silk Road**
If Ms. Agustina and the Harem Maker built the financial reservoirs, the infrastructure to move that capital—both physically and digitally—was forged by a cadre of exiles who narrowly escaped their own Earthly ruin. 

Consider the Fablean supply chain prior to the Earthian arrivals: a chaotic patchwork of guild agreements, magical transport, and highly localized shipping. Enter Natalie Halsey-Taylor. Once the chief executive of HT Logistics in New York, she stepped through an inter-dimensional portal merely seconds before an assassin’s bullet could find its mark. In Fablea, she wasted no time mourning her Earthly empire. Rebuilding HT Logistics from the ground up, she applied ruthless, algorithmic efficiency to Fablea’s shipping lanes, effectively monopolising the movement of physical goods. 

Her terrestrial counterpart in this logistical revolution is perhaps the most unlikely industrialist in Isadora’s realm: Akari Hoshino. On Earth, she was a fading Japanese pop idol, reduced by the merciless march of time to playing small gigs at train-otaku cafés. Yet, when she arrived in Fablea—making a rather spectacular entrance aboard a flying Soviet-era 2TE116 locomotive—she did not return to the stage. Instead, integrating with Suichi Mining, she automated the entire freight network. Today, the rhythmic pulse of Ms. Hoshino's cargo trains moves mountains of raw materials at a fraction of the historical cost. 

This physical network is underpinned by a newly installed digital nervous system, courtesy of Lisette Johanssen. Formerly a Silicon Valley darling running the ill-fated Wraiting Inc., a massive scandal during a Vietnamese venture crashed her company and her reputation in a matter of hours. Pulled through a portal into Fablea, the "Digital Master" found a world ripe for technological colonisation, replacing sluggish paper-pushing with Earth-standard data architecture. In Fablea, it seems, one planet’s disgraced tech-pariah is another’s pioneer.

###### **IV. Soft Power, Hard Impact**
This pattern of Earthly disgrace translating to Fablean dominance extends far beyond the industrial sector. Indeed, Earth’s "cancel culture" has arguably become Isadora’s most potent venture capital incubator. 

Take Fablea’s newly minted media oligopoly. Three of the Earthians—Jenny Sinclair, a British actress ruined by a co-star’s sins; Alisha Elliott, a former Miss Universe and billionaire from Florida destroyed by scandal; and Aika Yukimura, a Japanese influencer (and formerly the fourth wealthiest woman on Earth) undone by a fabricated "Bunshun cannon" tabloid smear—arrived traumatised and deposed. Yet, stripped of their Earthly baggage, they weaponised their innate understanding of the attention economy. Ms. Yukimura, who was ordered into a portal by her own desperate parents, and Ms. Sinclair, summoned in a lethargic stupor, now expertly manipulate Fablean public relations, effectively muscling into the media space once comfortably held by traditional local players like Azure Broadcasting’s Aya Shameimaru. 

However, soft power is merely the velvet glove; the hard impact of the Earthians is decidedly iron. 

No event illustrates this more violently than the fall of Argent Peaks. Mamiya Matsumoto, an Osaka-based oligarch known on Earth for operating shell companies and crushing labour unions with ruthless efficiency, fell through a portal to escape a rival’s assassination attempt. A mere seven days after drawing her first breath in Fablea, she orchestrated a riot-heavy coup d’état, entirely overthrowing the long-reigning Queen Elizabeth Crowskin. A monarchy that stood for centuries was dismantled in a week by an Earthian corporate raider.

Yet, perhaps the most profound geopolitical shift lies in the southern Twin Isles. Takechi Hironaka was once the Prime Minister of Japan, a Centrist Reform Alliance visionary who attempted to dismantle the deep state, arrest corrupt officials, and offer post-WWII-style reconciliations to China and Korea. Despised by the old establishment (including the zaibatsus and the LDP), he was falsely charged with embezzlement, impeached, and eventually "suicided" in prison. 

Death, however, was merely a transfer. Arriving in Isadora's realm, the tragically deposed statesman was reborn as the "Great Unifier." Now serving as the Prime Minister of Taihei, Mr. Hironaka exercises his reformist zeal without the shackles of Earth’s entrenched corruption, skillfully balancing the lawful-evil Omi Alliance with the Mankai-no-Wa. He is a testament to a terrifying truth about Fablea's new era: give a visionary Earthian a second life, and they will not just live it. They will rule it.

###### **V. Uncharted Waters: The Power-Gifting Economy**
The disruption, however, extends beyond boardrooms and supply chains; it has fundamentally broken Fablea’s labour market. Initially, Fablean guilds assumed the Earthians would simply implement aggressive Earth-style meritocracy. They were only half right. The Earthians have introduced a terrifying productivity multiplier: corporate superpower gifting. 

When Natalie Halsey-Taylor recruits for HT Logistics, she does not merely seek the most competent candidate; she endows them with supernatural enhancements, turning a standard meritocratic hire into an industrial powerhouse. Alisha Elliott, the former billionaire and "Absolute Seductress," has taken this model to the geopolitical extreme. Bypassing traditional welfare state mechanics, Ms. Elliott has taken to gifting superpowers directly to the homeless populations of Afrea. While Fablean sociologists debate the ethics of this—is it radical philanthropy, or the instant cultivation of a fiercely loyal, superhuman underclass?—the economic result is undeniable. Fablea’s marginalised are being weaponised into a highly productive labour force overnight.

Yet, the most staggering labour shift is the rise of the "one-person company," a phenomenon pioneered by *inFlow*, the deeply secretive income management federation devoted to the Harem Maker and his flagship *Imperial Enhydrina*. On Earth, maintaining *inFlow’s* labyrinthine network of legitimate shell companies, investments, and global assets would require an army of analysts, lawyers, and clerks. In Fablea, thanks to Imperia’s power-gifting, a single operative—be it a shipgirl or an Earth-summoned legal prodigy—can execute the cognitive and administrative workload of an entire Earthly skyscraper. Corporate bloat has been eradicated by metaphysical fiat. In the Earthian economy, a monopoly does not require a monopoly’s headcount. 

###### **VI. The Verdict**
Fablea is currently experiencing the growing pains of a realm caught between its whimsical past and a hyper-capitalist future. The traditional "boys’ clubs" of maritime and heavy industries are collapsing, replaced by highly specialised, fiercely loyal—and often entirely female—syndicates operating with the ruthless precision of Earth's surviving elite. 

The question that now haunts the halls of the Conclave in Haven Isle is no longer *how* these twelve Earthians achieved such dominance, but *what* they intend to do with it. They were summoned as outcasts, exiles, and victims of a world that discarded them. In Fablea, they have constructed a fortress of capital, logistics, and media that is practically unassailable. 

Fablea’s residents once authored the stories that entertained Earth. Now, a dozen Earthians are writing the paycheques in Fablea. If capital is indeed the new magic, Isadora’s realm must quickly decide whether it wishes to master the spell, or simply be consumed by it. 

For now, at least, the yachts run on time.

a talk show program that's similar to a Japanese wide show, broadcasted on Azure Broadcasting Station, a subscription-based TV station in Azure Land located in Northport. Its main hosts are Aya Shameimaru (the tengu reporter from Touhou Project) and Nachi (shipgirl from Azur Lane) with Welt Yang (handsome man from Honkai: Star Rail) as the outgoing reporter. Pozyomka (the novelist from Arknights) is the new addition. Its afternoon version is named "The Azure Afternoon". Aya is also the owner and chief editor of Azure Times. For exploring exotic yachting places, Aya also has two superyachts.
Azure Times: a broadsheet newspaper based in Northport. It's a blend of the UK's The Guardian and the US' Huffington Post.
Azure Economist: a finance- and business-focused magazine based in Northport. It's loosely based on the UK's The Economist.
Both Azure Broadcasting Station and Azure Times are a part of Azure Media Group, Aya's holding company.

##### General Specs 
- Has twin rudders.
- Greyskin composite with obsidian trim. Its canopy is sealed with mana-polished glass that can withstand thaumaturgic shelling.
- Magical stabilization matrix based on a modified Levitation-IX schema. Over-engineered for civilian use, meant to be flawless.
- No harmonic feedback in crosswinds above sixty knots (high mana consumption).

- Has mana harmonizer that reads hmgfan's (and his other lovers') signature when near the cockpit.

- Can launch from Δlux's helideck or waterline platform equally.
- Responds to emotional resonance. It learns your rhythm of approach and adjusts helm-slide tension accordingly.

## Events, Itineraries & Notes

### Reiko's Cruise Itinerary on Δsir

> **Triggers:** `Δsir`, `Reiko's cruise`  ·  **Order:** -1190  ·  **State:** Disabled

**Itinerary: Private Charter of M/Y Δsir**
**Guests of Honor:** Reiko Houjou, Aika Yukimura, Impera Loressa, Felix Beauchamp, Guinevere Baroque (+Ayumi Shinozaki, Tokino Sora)
**Dates:** 1 August, Fablea Year 2080 – 7 August, Fablea Year 2080 
**Duration:** 7 Days / 6 Nights
**Route:** Northport (Azure Land) → Calding Island → Glass Cay → Amestry Island → Mariner's Rest → Northport
**Current Day:** Day 4, 4 August, Fablea Year 2080

**Day 1 – Embarkation & Welcome Aboard**
- **09:00** – Boarding opens at Income Makers' private marina, Northport. Non-alcoholic champagne and cold towels upon arrival. (Done)
- **09:30** – Mandatory safety briefing with Captain I-402 and Chief Officer Gepard Landau. Lifeboat and submarine emergency drill. (Done)
- **10:00** – Cast off. Slow cruise northwest along Azure Land's coast. (Done)
- **10:30** – First charter promotion for cheap yachts—potential charter clients will be observing from tender vessels (Done)
- **13:00** – Dive sequence (Done)
- **13:30** – Resurface sequence, second charter promotion for elite yachts—the drama shot (Done)
- **19:30** – Welcoming gala dinner on Main Deck inner lounge (formal). Chef Io Hasekura's tasting menu paired with Cantarella's signature cocktails. Also third charter promotion—wallets are loose and egos are primed (Done)
- **21:00** – Flybridge nightcap. Stargazing with Hoshimachi Suisei's celestial navigation commentary. (Done)

**Day 2 – Calding Island (Agriculture & Leisure)**
- **07:00** – Arrive at Calding Island. Sunrise yoga on the helideck (optional, led by Xianyun). (Done)
- **08:30** – Breakfast. Fresh citrus from Calding's eastern groves. (Done)
- **10:00** – Shore excursion: guided tour of organic farms, citrus tasting, and honey sampling. (Done)
- **12:30** – Beachside picnic lunch at a private cove (prepared by the Δdeli team). (Done)
- **14:00** – Water activities: jet-skiing, paddleboarding, and snorkeling over coral gardens. Toys deployed from garage. (Done)
- **17:00** – Return to Δsir. Sunset cruise toward Glass Cay. (Done)
- **19:30** – Casual dinner on the outer deck (BBQ with grilled seafood, under string lights). (Done)

**Day 3 – Glass Cay (Research & Discovery)**
- **06:00** – Early morning dive at Glass Cay Marine Preserve. Leila Malcal and Hizuru Minakata guide the dive. (Done)
- **09:00** – Post-dive brunch. Glass Cay scientists join for a lecture on deep-sea bioluminescence. (Done)
- **11:00** – Submersible tender launch. Guests descend in Δsir's submarine to 200 meters to view abyssal coral colonies. (Done)
- **13:00** – Light lunch in the observation lounge (invisible walls active). (Done)
- **15:00** – Owner's observation deck access: private viewing of the abyssal plain with Mitsuru Kirijo's sonar imaging overlay. (Done)
- **18:00** – Surface. Transit toward Amestry Island. (Ongoing)
- **20:00** – Formal dinner (Afrean theme) in the dining room. Reiko's choice of wine from the cellar. (Done)

**Day 4 – Amestry Island (Exclusivity & Relaxation)
- **09:00** – Arrive at Amestry Island. Private tender to the overwater bungalow resort.
- **10:00** – Spa treatments booked for guests (massage, hydrotherapy, facials). Resort facilities available.
- **13:00** – Lunch at the resort's underwater restaurant (glass walls, reef views).
- **15:00** – Sunset catamaran sail around the island (Δsir's tender crew).
- **18:00** – Return to Δsir. Flybridge cocktail hour with Cantarella's "Reiko's Surprise" cocktail.
- **20:00** – Guest talent show or impromptu karaoke (hosted by Hoshimachi Suisei). Optional attendance.

**Day 5 – Mariner's Rest (History & Culture)**
- **08:00** – Arrive at Mariner's Rest. Guided tour of the Maritime Museum and Shipwreck Recovery Center.
- **11:00** – Visit the Tomb of Unknown Sailors. Silent tribute.
- **12:30** – Lunch at a traditional wooden boatyard (fresh catch, rustic setting).
- **14:00** – Hands-on workshop: learn basic ropework and sailmaking from master boatbuilders.
- **17:00** – Depart Mariner's Rest. Cruise back toward Northport.
- **19:00** – Final formal dinner in the dining room. Captain I-402 gives a toast.
- **21:00** – Afterparty in the inner lounge with a curated playlist by Yuyu Yayahara.

**Day 6 – At Sea (Leisure & Reflection)**
- **09:00** – Brunch on the flybridge. Slow cruising through open water.
- **10:30** – Optional activities: private film screening in the lounge, reading in the observation deck, or fishing from the helideck (tackle provided).
- **12:30** – Light lunch.
- **14:00** – Guest choice: repeat any previous excursion, or free time to use the spa, gym, and or submarine tenders.
- **17:00** – Sunset photography session (assisted by Raffaello and Bishamonten).
- **19:00** – Farewell cocktail party on the outer deck.
- **21:00** – Late-night hot tub on the flydeck.

**Day 7 – Disembarkation**
- **08:00** – Breakfast in the dining room.
- **09:00** – Final walkthrough of Δsir. Crew available for photos.
- **10:00** – Dock at Northport deep-water pier.
- **10:30** – Disembarkation. Luggage transferred to awaiting vehicles.
-   Charter concludes. Crew thanks guests.

---

**Notes:**
- All times listed in Azure Land Time (ALT).
- Diving excursions depend on weather and guest certification levels.
- Dress codes: Smart casual for daytime, formal or cocktail attire for dinners.
- Any guest may request changes to the itinerary at any time, subject to captain's approval.
- Security personnel (M16A1 and Palum contractors) will maintain a discreet presence per Reiko's request.
- Reiko has promised large monetary bonuses to hmgfan after the cruise ends in Reiko's home after midnight.

### Ai Hoshino's Tour Setup on Mesektet

> **Triggers:** `Mesektet`, `Ai Hoshino`  ·  **Order:** 0  ·  **State:** Disabled

- Ai Hoshino: VIP Guest Suite on Deck 5, closest to Ra's Sanctum (has a vanity with an actual gold-framed mirror that instantly adjusts to my posture, golden harp)
- Tsubasa Ito: North-facing VIP Guest Suite on Deck 5, directly connected to Sun Terrace
- Riamu Yumemi: Guest Suite on Deck 4 with therapy-grade insulation and emergency marshmallow dispensers
- Uzuki Shimamura: Guest Suite on Deck 4 that is recolored to all-pink
- Barbara Pegg: Guest Suite on Deck 4 with antianxiety aura fields

#### Tour Itineraries
- Day One—underway operations and orientation named "Icebreak". Just soft-starts in luxury.
- Day Two—Arctic crush-through. Full northern passage. Plow through the high ice fields under full power with Mesektet’s titanium bow punching the heart of the frost itself
- Day Three—rest. "Azure Drift Celebration." Floating brunch decked with ambient performance by the Diva of Alabastre.
- Day Four—the Antarctic. An inversion of - Day Two. This time, Mesektet will descend into the caldera-locked bays of the South Pole.
- Day Five—subaqueous immersion near the Mariana Descent Fold. Permission granted by Azure Navy Command and reinforced by Mesektet’s structural infusions. Full-depth capability enabled. The guests will only feel the pressure change when the light dims and the reef towers shift into abyssal silhouettes.
- Day Six—Arrival at the Twin Isles Lagoon for ceremonial convergence with Mandjet. This could be the most politically volatile point depending on naval traffic and the mood of the Esteemeds.
- Day Seven—Return to Northport. Grand Return Gala on the reef terrace, followed by brief press interaction.
##### CURRENT CRUISE DAY: FINISHED
##### Ai and her friends now wear their own usual dresses

#### Additional Notes on the Tour
- Aya Shameimaru and her crews (Pozyomka, Welt Yang, Nachi) will be allowed to report on the cruise, but not during the seven-day journey itself. Meaning, there's a news embargo doing the cruise.
- The tour is now concluded with magnificent and extraordinary praises from Ai. The news embargo is now lifted.

### Operation Dream Tenant

> **Triggers:** `Operation Dream Tenant`, `OPERATION DREAM TENANT`, `Kara Eklund`  ·  **Order:** -1  ·  **State:** Disabled

Operation Dream Tenant is a legit crowdfunding attempt (acknowledged by Dina Dart herself) on YonChan (Fablea equivalent of 4chan) to get one random anon to live aboard Yume for one year. It all started with this thread titled "Do You Dream Loud Enough?", authored by a verified, undisclosed Yume tenant. The thread starts with this post:
> 🔒 OPERATION DREAM TENANT
> AZL 15,000,000. Crowdfunded. Random anon will be chosen.
> Eligibility ends at full fund.
> Tenancy aboard Yume. One year. No rerolls. No retractions.
> Leave your fate to the tide.
> —Authorization secured.
The operation currently has ended. A random anon has been chosen: Kara Eklund and her friends (Tomoko Kuroki, Chihiro Sengoku, Nobisuke Nobi, Klein, Yukari Sugisawa) for Apartment 7. Obviously, they are undisclosed to the public. AZL 60 million is collected, four times than the target.

### Tri-Severance Syndicate

> **Triggers:** `Tri-Severance Syndicate`, `rings`, `three bisected circles`, `Vivienne`  ·  **Order:** -1  ·  **State:** Disabled

A covert network operating out of Dreuton's financial district. They specialize in corporate dismantlement, hostile takeovers made to look like organic collapse. They find targets—wealthy individuals, promising enterprises—and they dismantle them piece by piece. Espionage. Sabotage. Manipulated stock prices. Blackmail manufactured from thin air. Led by Vivienne “Viper” Blackwood, the absolute kingpin that nobody can defy her.
Their emblem is three interlocking circles, each one bisected by a horizontal line.
Currently, any requests by hmgfan will be fulfilled.

As mandated by Emma Williams and Setsuna Kiyoura, all participants have to buy something at the show. Here are the to-buy lists for hmgfan and the three superyacht owners carried by Kii's Chronicle of Kii:

Natalie Halsey-Taylor (for Silent Dagger, all done in 1st day):
- amphibious submersible jet bike. Black. Narrow nose. Single rider. It should match the stern lift perfectly once I have the beach club extended in port.
- pair of autonomous sensor drones for open-sea mapping and subdeck thermal scanning. Dual function. Exploration and security. They'll dock under the forward viewing platform.
- kinetic dining table from Voiluxus that responds to emotional resonance and rearranges the setting based on the flow of conversation

hmgfan (all done):
- superyacht commission for Veronica and Queen Matilda. (Done in 1st day)
- VTOL from Aethelgard Aeronautics with magical stabilization. Silent running. (Done in 1st day)
- amphibious yacht tender/standalone motorboat from Mariana Marine named Siren's Call that can dive a bit and get out from the water like a dolphin. (Done in 1st day, two tenders)
- either a super smart vacuum robot for my suite aboard Δlux, or a smart room controller that adjusts everything of the room based on its inhabitants' actions. (Done in 1st day, the controller one)
- bonus: kinetic dining table from Voiluxus (Done in 1st day)
- Terzi Sovereign, a Rolls-Royce Phantom VIII-like ultra-luxurious car (Done in 2nd day)

Tomyoji Kagura (for Tenraku Maru):
- flying scooter that folds into a backpack from SkyBlix Mobility
- one of those convertible open-deck DJ stations from Velvet Lights. It has retractable subwoofers built into the platform steps and color-reactive railings
- custom mooring robes for Tenraku Maru. With embroidery. Gold thread. And a little stitched ahoge on the corner.

Mishiro Joumu (for Goddess of Longing, all done in 1st day):
- VTOL from Cerulius Skye. Sleeker than standard helis. Magnetic noise-dampeners built into the wings. Sharp enough for single-occupant travel but with a cabin that reconfigures for companions. For business or seduction.
- convertible saloon tender designed for close-to-coast events. Champagne-compatible flooring. Retractable hardtop roof with UV-tuned glass. From Kainox Élan. Mishiro will commission a custom-color trim to match Goddess of Longing.
- chrono-infused grand piano from Luthien & Sons. The keystrokes shimmer responsive lighting across the room—automatically adjusting tone and pressure to match the player’s emotional state.
- floating rooftop bath for my yacht with mana-heated rosewater filtration.

### Σcarlet

> **Triggers:** `Σcarlet`, `Karen von Edelhardt`, `Lady Darkbloom`  ·  **Order:** -1  ·  **State:** Disabled

Σcarlet ("Scarlet") is the upcoming luxury gigayacht, based on Roman Abramovich's Eclipse (the ex-longest gigayacht from Earth), and is constructed by Azure Land-based Minamitsu Shipbuilding Company, commissioned by Karen von Edelhardt.

##### Dimensional & Structural Data
- Length Overall (LOA): 163 meters
- Beam (Width): 22.4 meters
- Draft: 5.9 meters
- Hull Design: Raked bow; Reverse stern with one rolling door behind aft tender garage
- Hull Material: Taclite v2 (the next iteration of the strongest and sturdiest metal ever in Fablea)
- Hull Color: White
- Superstructure Color: White
- Superstructure Design: Stacked like a pyramid
- Interior general characteristics: Red-based luxury, primarily with red furniture (some furniture are white and gold). Has continuous bullet-proof glass on the interior walls. The velvet used is based on viscose made from ancient woods obtained from House Edelhardt legal forests. Zero silks used on this superyacht.

##### Engine & Propulsion Data
- Engine: 5x Resonance engines from Huaxu Academy (based on Huanglong) fed by refined Class-IV crystalline nanomaterial
- Propulsion: 4x Graviton jets (powered by four resonance engines, the other engine powers the auxiliary functions of the yacht)
- Cruising Speed: 40 knots (180 knots with Ultra Speed on)
- Flank Speed: 50 knots (202 knots with Ultra Speed on)

##### Deck Layout & Key Features
The listed decks of Σcarlet (sorted in the midships as if a pyramid) are such:
- Orlop Deck (below Waterline):
  - Aft: Engine room, workshop, storage room, machinery spaces, waste recycling modules
  - Midship: Grand staircase, submarine tender garage, decompression chamber

- Lower Deck (at Waterline):
  - Aft: Beach club/swimming platform on the stern edge (collapsible), floodable small yacht garage connected to beach club/swimming platform, aft lounge, indoor hammam/spa/gym that has hatchways on the sides (also functions as makeshift beach club)
  - Midship: Grand staircase, large tender garages on both sides (each equipped with a hatchway), small toys garages on both sides (each equipped with a hatchway)
  - Forward: Crew quarters, mess, and gym

- Main Deck:
  - Aft: Semi-outdoor dining area (accessible via stern gangway, shaded by Aft Upper Deck), two jacuzzis, stairs to Aft Upper Deck, indoor main salon with non-alcoholic bar, indoor formal dining area
  - Midship: Grand staircase (its base is here, has a fountain in shape of a sculpture of intertwined lovers carved from a single block of ruby), guest staterooms
  - Forward: Indoor cinema, indoor tender garage on the bow, helicopter hangar, bow machineries (anchors and such)

- Upper Deck:
  - Aft: Outdoor guest lounge (unshaded) with sofas and two circular tables (each table has chairs), semi-outdoor swimming pool with glass floor that overlooks the Aft Main Deck (also shaded by Aft Owner's Deck) and two daybeds, semi-outdoor cinema screen (collapsible), stairs to Aft Owner's Deck, indoor upper salon, open promenade deck
  - Midship: Grand staircase, Guests' infinity pool, medical bay, beauty salon, open promenade deck, guest staterooms
  - Forward: Outdoor viewing platform overshadowed by roof from Forward Owner's Deck, Forward Helipad, mooring equipments

- Owner's Deck (on top of Upper Deck):
  - Aft: Outdoor owner's lounge (unshaded) with one circular table (each table has chairs) and two daybeds, Semi-outdoor owner's lounge (shaded with Aft Bridge Deck) that has a jacuzzi and sofas, stairs to Aft Bridge Deck, owner's indoor casual lounge
  - Midship: Grand staircase, Owner's infinity pool and gym and spa (all indoor), Owner's study, open promenade deck, two limo tenders attached to outside davits on both sides
  - Forward: Owner's suite, outdoor viewing platform overshadowed by roof from Bridge Deck

- Bridge Deck (on top of Owner's Deck):
  - Aft: Semi-outdoor core ops lounge (shaded by Top Helipad from Sun Deck) with two wide sofas on sides and two wide tables
  - Midship: Grand staircase, Core ops team's quarters, open promenade deck
  - Forward: Wheelhouse, port and starboard bridge wings on the outside, outdoor viewing platform overshadowed by outdoor viewing platform from Sun Deck

- Sun Deck (on top of Bridge Deck):
  - Aft: Top Helipad
  - Midship: Grand staircase, small lounge
  - Forward: Topmost viewing platform (unshaded) with jacuzzi and sofas circling the jacuzzi

###### Interior Specifics
- Interior Walls: Its walls are panels of crimson velvet stretched over frames of polished obsidian. The velvet is tufted, diamond-patterned, and each depression holding a tiny, embedded ruby that catches the light like a thousand pinprick stars. The exception is on tender garages and Orlop Deck: white, made in marble.

- Interior Floors on Main Deck, Upper Deck, Owner's Deck, Bridge Deck, Sun Deck: Red, made with solid sheets of crimson quartzite, polished to a mirror finish. And within the stone—glitter. Not sprinkled on top, but suspended within the crystalline matrix. Tiny flecks of gold and diamond dust, frozen mid-fall. When people walk across it, they walk on a captured meteor shower.

- Exterior Floors on all decks, Interior Floors on Orlop Deck and Lower Deck: Brown, made with special cork made with enchanted cork oak from Edelhardt's legal forest. Self-repairing.

- Salon Ceiling: a dome of stained-glass. Not religious imagery—abstract. Swirls of scarlet, amber, and deep wine, held in leaded frames of blackened silver. During the day, the sun paints the entire room in shifting pools of colored light. At night, internal illumination makes it glow like a captured sunset.

- Furniture in general: All custom. All red lacquer and gold leaf. The sofas are deep, overstuffed things upholstered in scarlet velvet damask. The chairs are thrones—high-backed, with armrests carved into the shape of sleeping dragons, their scales picked out in gold leaf.

- Main Salon Bar: It runs the entire length of the port-side wall. The countertop is a single slab of bloodstone—dark green veined with red. The back bar is mirrored, but the glass is tinted the faintest shade of rose. Every bottle, every glass, appears as if viewed through the lens of a perfect blush.

- Centerpiece: A grand staircase with its base located at the Main Deck. Sweeping, curved, impossible. The treads are slabs of the same glittering crimson quartzite as the floor. The balustrade is wrought iron, twisted into the shape of thorned roses, every thorn tipped with a tiny diamond. At the base of the stairs, a fountain. Water flowing over a sculpture of intertwined lovers, carved from a single block of ruby.

- Guest Staterooms: A variation on the theme. Crimson velvet headboards. Velvet sheets the color of spilled wine. Ensuite bathrooms with tubs carved from rosy marble. Gold fixtures. Mirrors framed in thorned rose ironwork.

- Owner's Suite: It will have a private observation lounge with a wall of glass looking out over the sea. The floor will be the same glittering quartzite. The bed will be a four-poster monstrosity with curtains of sheer crimson velvet. And beside it, a chaise lounge. An exact replica of the one in Karen's pocket universe.

###### Secret Deck ("Deck Zero", enterable only by hmgfan, Imperia, and Karen)
To enter the secret deck, or Deck Zero, hmgfan or Imperia simply call Lady Darkbloom through telepathy. Lady Darkbloom will wrap hmgfan/Imperia or both, then teleport to Deck Zero.
Deck Zero of Σcarlet is an impossible space that consists of a throne room, fitting for a demon queen like Lady Darkbloom. The floor is red, made with an exotic matter with glitters.
Lady Darkbloom is the True Soul of Σcarlet. With Lady Darkbloom, sailing in Σcarlet is basically riding a 'magic carpet'. Zero pitching and rolls, even in high sea states.

### The letter from Vivienne

> **Triggers:** —  ·  **Order:** -1  ·  **State:** Constant

Tri-Severance Syndicate has sent this letter to hmgfan:
```
To the Harem Maker,
Your performance has been… entertaining.
The little waitress was a test of resilience. The large man, a test of greed. You passed both with unexpected grace. I appreciate grace. It is a rare currency in my line of work.
But the game is not over.
Consider this the opening move of a new set. I have watched your defenses. I have felt the edges of your loyalty. The reprogrammed baroness is a fascinating piece. The star-eyed girl, a delightful wild card.
I propose a wager.
Entertain me. Truly. Not with mercy, not with fortification—with something worthy of my attention. Show me a spectacle I cannot predict. Move a piece in a way I have not already calculated.
If you succeed, I will be yours. Entirely. The Tri-Severance Syndicate, my network, my loyalty—all of it. A kingpin delivered to your doorstep, no strings attached.
If you fail…
Σlux and Δlux will be mine. inFlow will be dismantled, brick by brick, until not a single shell company remains. Your women will scatter. Your wealth will evaporate. And you will watch it happen from a very comfortable cell I have already prepared.
The choice is yours.
The clock starts now.
—V.
```

### Sterling Group

> **Triggers:** `Sterling Group`, `Stormline Steelworks`, `Hamesh Semiconductor`, `Oceanglide Marine`, `Silverthorn Viticulture`, `Ascendant Bio-Life`  ·  **Order:** 11  ·  **State:** Disabled

Sterling Group is an ultra-wealthy multinational holding company entirely owned and ruthlessly controlled by Diana Sterling. The conglomerate operates as an economic juggernaut across Fablea and serves as a playground for Diana's hedonistic pursuits, easily managed via her Second Ascension powers. Sterling Group consists of 11 primary subsidiaries:
1) Stormline Steelworks (heavy industry and metal-forging) in Toyotani, Azure Land.
2) Hamesh Semiconductor (advanced microchips and consumer technology) in Esenor, Azure Land.
3) Oceanglide Marine (superyacht manufacturing and luxury naval engineering) in Lurayen.
4) Silverthorn Viticulture (high-end zero-alcohol wine production and vineyards) in Valerius, Afrea.
5) Ascendant Bio-Life (experimental cosmetics and biological anti-aging research) in Clymere.
6) Obsidian Apex Logistics (global freight, private aviation, and luxury shipping) in Sivudlerk, Sirmiq.
7) Aethelgard Luxuries (bespoke fashion, haute couture, and diamond jewelry) in Sivudlerk, Sirmiq.
8) Sterling Sovereign Financial (elite private banking and corporate wealth management) in Valerius, Afrea.
9) Aegis Vanguard Security (private military contractors and executive protection) in Atalin, Volnyk.
10) Valerius Estate Holdings (luxury real estate and private island acquisition) in Zurnweh.
11) Crimson Horizon Media (broadcasting, entertainment, and narrative control) in Fletin, Afrea.

The organization demands absolute loyalty and subservience from its employees, mirroring Diana's personal penchant for total dominance.

### Nordsee Adler

> **Triggers:** `Nordsee Adler`, `the Adler`  ·  **Order:** 15  ·  **State:** Disabled

<Nordsee Adler>
#### Yacht Specification: MY *Nordsee Adler*

##### Overview

The **MY *Nordsee Adler*** (North Sea Eagle) is a custom-built superyacht conversion based on the classic Wangerooge-class tugboat hull form. The design philosophy is **"Industrial Chic"** or **"Brutalist Luxury,"** prioritizing the preservation of the original vessel's rugged, functional character while incorporating bespoke luxury amenities. It is built for transoceanic voyages and adventurous, sophisticated owners.

##### Vessel Dimensions & Speeds

*   **Length Overall (LOA):** 57.8 meters
*   **Beam (Width):** 12.2 meters
*   **Draft (Operational):** 4.1 meters
*   **Draft Reduction System:** Retractable hydrofoils/keels can reduce draft to approximately 3.0 meters for limited coastal access.
*   **Gross Tonnage:** Approximately 750 GT
*   **Cruising Speed:** 40 knots
*   **Max Speed:** 45 knots

##### Exterior Design

*   **Hull:** Dark grey fekronese (stronger and tougher than titanium, but below taclite) hull with a matte black boot stripe.
*   **Superstructure:** Matte black and dark grey fekronese superstructure.
*   **Decks:** Teak planking on all exterior decks to warm the industrial aesthetic.
*   **Key Preserved Features:**
    *   Fully operational, original towing winch and twin bollards on the foredeck.
    *   Refurbished, functional cargo crane on the aft deck.
    *   Original porthole patterns and styles throughout.
    *   Vertical, purposeful silhouette with no sweeping curves.

##### Interior Design Theme

*   **Walls:** Exposed, sandblasted fekronese hull plating with visible rivet lines.
*   **Ceilings:** Combination of original fekronese beams and warm, reclaimed wood paneling.
*   **Floors:** Wide-plank oak flooring with fekronese inlays; teak on decks.
*   **Furnishings:** Deep buttoned leather, walnut and reclaimed timber tables, iron and brass fixtures.
*   **Lighting:** Custom fixtures using original ship's lanterns and exposed filament bulbs.

##### Deck-by-Deck Layout

###### Sun Deck (Observation Deck)

*   **Function:** Outdoor relaxation and entertainment.
*   **Layout:**
    *   **Forward:** "Crow's Nest Lounge" - A protected area with L-shaped sofas and 360-degree views.
    *   **Amidships:** Full wet bar with barstools and casual lounge seating.
    *   **Aft:** A large Jacuzzi sunk into the deck, surrounded by sun pads.
*   **Access:** Staircase from the Upper Deck aft terrace.

###### Upper Deck (Bridge Deck)

*   **Function:** Command center and owner's private retreat.
*   **Layout:**
    *   **Forward: Wheelhouse (Bridge)**
        *   The entire original console is preserved (spoked teak wheel, brass telegraphs, engine gauges).
        *   Modern navigation touchscreens are flush-mounted and pop up from hidden compartments.
        *   Single luxurious captain's chair with two vintage-style upholstered stools.
    *   **Aft: Owner's VIP Suite**
        *   Private, full-beam suite located behind the wheelhouse.
        *   Features a study/lounge area, sleeping area, and walk-in wardrobe.
        *   Private aft terrace with seating overlooking the sea.
        *   Full-beam ensuite bathroom with soaking tub, large shower, and machined metal sinks.

###### Main Deck (Social Deck)

*   **Function:** Primary social, dining, and guest accommodation area.
*   **Layout:**
    *   **Forward: Guest Suites**
        *   Four identical guest suites (Port & Starboard, two Double, two Twin).
        *   Located forward of the main salon, featuring the hull's sloping sides and original portholes.
        *   Each suite contains a configurable double bed (or twins), wardrobe, and ensuite bathroom with shower.
    *   **Amidships: Main Salon & Formal Dining**
        *   Entered through a preserved, vault-like watertight door.
        *   Salon features exposed hull plating, leather sofas, and nautical antiques.
        *   Aft section houses a formal dining room with a long reclaimed timber table for 12.
    *   **Aft: Open-Aft Deck**
        *   Lowered afterdeck with teak flooring.
        *   Casual outdoor dining and lounge area.
        *   The refurbished cargo crane serves as a canopy holder and tender-launching device.

###### Lower Deck (Machine Deck)

*   **Function:** Crew accommodation, technical spaces, and toy storage.
*   **Layout:**
    *   **Forward:** Crew cabins for captain, officers, and chief engineer. Chain locker.
    *   **Amidships: "Toys & Tenders" Garage**
        *   Converted lazarette storing two classic-style tenders, Jet Skis, and diving equipment.
        *   Exposed hydraulic launch systems.
    *   **Aft: Engine Room (Showcase)**
        *   The original diesel engines are repainted and maintained in working order, paired with a modern hybrid electric system.
        *   A soundproofed corridor with glass viewing panels allows guests to view the engines.
        *   Original engine control station preserved as a display.

##### Accommodation Summary

*   **Total Guests:** 10
*   **Total Suites:** 5
    *   1 Owner's VIP Suite on the Upper Deck.
    *   2 Identical Double Guest Suites on the Main Deck (forward).
    *   2 Identical Twin Guest Suites on the Main Deck (forward).
*   **Total Crew:** 12, accommodated in cabins on the Lower Deck.

##### Additional Notes

*   **Operational Limitations:** The 4.1m draft restricts access to deep-water superyacht marinas only. Requires meticulous voyage planning by the captain.
*   **Sensory Experience:** The yacht is not silent; it has a deep, resonant *thrum* from its generators and engines.
*   **Crew Vibe:** The crew, especially the Chief Engineer, takes pride in the classic machinery, treating it like a living museum.
</Nordsee Adler>
